```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
pcap_analyzer.py  -  보안컴퓨터네트워크 패킷 분석 도우미
=====================================================================
 * 외부 라이브러리 필요 없음 (파이썬 3.7 이상, 표준 라이브러리만 사용)
 * pcap / pcapng 지원
 * 링크 계층: Ethernet, Cisco HDLC(GNS3 시리얼 링크 캡처), Raw IP,
              Linux cooked(SLL/SLL2), Loopback, PPP

사용법 (이것 하나뿐, 옵션 없음)
  python pcap_analyzer.py 폴더

  → 폴더 안 pcap 전부 분석 → 목록 화면. 키 하나로 동작, 화면 맨 아래에 쓸 수 있는 키가 항상 표시됨
    ↑↓ 고르기   Enter 열기   ←→ 탭/이전·다음 패킷   q 뒤로(첫 화면에서는 종료)   ? 도움말
    목록 화면   숫자 = 그 파일 열기, d 전체 답안, c 계정·비번, f Wireshark 필터, s 글자 검색, w txt 저장
    파일 화면   탭 1 답안 2 중요 패킷 3 전체 패킷 4 TCP 대화 5 통계 6 파일 추출,  [ ] 이전/다음 파일
    패킷 해부   바이트를 계층·필드별 색으로 칠하고, 옆에 색의 의미와 필드 해석을 보여줌

판별하는 것
  [침투 테스트 도구]  ARTEX(AI 자율 침투 테스트: artex-enrich/norma UA, 8787 포트, LLM API 접속),
    nmap(-sS/-sT/-sU/-sX/-sF/-sN/-sA, -sn 호스트 탐색, -O OS 탐지, -sV 버전 탐지, -sC/-A NSE),
    Hydra / Medusa / Ncrack (FTP·SSH·HTTP·Telnet 무차별 대입 + 성공 여부, SSH는 패킷 크기로 성공 판정),
    Nikto, sqlmap, dirb, Gobuster, ffuf, wfuzz, WPScan, Nuclei, Acunetix 등 웹 스캐너(User-Agent),
    hping3(SYN Flood/Land/스캔), Metasploit(4444, Meterpreter), 리버스/바인드 셸,
    vsftpd 2.3.4·UnrealIRCd·Samba usermap·distcc 백도어(Metasploitable)
  [웹 공격]  SQL 인젝션, XSS, 경로 탐색/LFI, RFI, 명령 주입, Shellshock, Log4Shell, 웹셸, SSTI
  [기본]  FTP/Telnet/POP3/IMAP/SMTP/HTTP 계정·비밀번호, SSH, ping, traceroute/tracert,
    nslookup(DNS), TLS 접속 도메인(SNI), SYN/ICMP/UDP Flood, Land, Ping of Death, Smurf,
    Teardrop, ARP 스푸핑, DNS 스푸핑, DHCP 고갈, MAC 플러딩
  ※ 실제 Kali 도구 트래픽으로 지문을 검증했고, Wireshark 필터 결과는 tshark와 대조 검증함
"""

import base64
import collections
import copy
import datetime
import glob
import gzip
import os
import re
import struct
import sys
import unicodedata
import urllib.parse
import zlib

# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 1. 캡처 파일 읽기 (pcap / pcapng)
# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
LINKTYPES = {0: 'Loopback(BSD)', 1: 'Ethernet', 9: 'PPP', 12: 'Raw IP', 14: 'Raw IP',
             50: 'PPP-HDLC', 101: 'Raw IP', 104: 'Cisco HDLC (GNS3 시리얼)',
             108: 'Loopback', 113: 'Linux SLL', 228: 'Raw IPv4', 229: 'Raw IPv6',
             276: 'Linux SLL2'}


def read_capture(path):
    """[(타임스탬프, 링크타입, 바이트), ...] 반환"""
    with open(path, 'rb') as f:
        data = f.read()
    if len(data) < 24:
        raise ValueError('파일이 너무 작음')
    magic = data[:4]
    if magic == b'\x0a\x0d\x0d\x0a':
        return read_pcapng(data)
    if magic in (b'\xd4\xc3\xb2\xa1', b'\x4d\x3c\xb2\xa1'):
        e = '<'
    elif magic in (b'\xa1\xb2\xc3\xd4', b'\xa1\xb2\x3c\x4d'):
        e = '>'
    else:
        raise ValueError('pcap/pcapng 파일이 아님')
    nano = magic in (b'\x4d\x3c\xb2\xa1', b'\xa1\xb2\x3c\x4d')
    linktype = struct.unpack(e + 'I', data[20:24])[0] & 0x0FFFFFFF
    out, off, n = [], 24, len(data)
    while off + 16 <= n:
        ts, frac, incl, _orig = struct.unpack(e + 'IIII', data[off:off + 16])
        off += 16
        incl = min(incl, n - off)
        out.append((ts + frac / (1e9 if nano else 1e6), linktype, data[off:off + incl], _orig))
        off += incl
    return out


def read_pcapng(data):
    out, off, n, e, ifaces = [], 0, len(data), '<', []
    while off + 12 <= n:
        try:
            if data[off:off + 4] == b'\x0a\x0d\x0d\x0a':          # Section Header
                e = '<' if data[off + 8:off + 12] == b'\x4d\x3c\x2b\x1a' else '>'
                ifaces = []
            btype, blen = struct.unpack(e + 'II', data[off:off + 8])
            if blen < 12 or off + blen > n:
                break
            body = data[off + 8:off + blen - 4]
            if btype == 1:                                         # Interface Description
                lt = struct.unpack(e + 'H', body[:2])[0]
                res, o = 1e-6, 8
                while o + 4 <= len(body):
                    code, ln = struct.unpack(e + 'HH', body[o:o + 4])
                    o += 4
                    if code == 0:
                        break
                    if code == 9 and ln >= 1 and o < len(body):
                        v = body[o]
                        res = 2.0 ** -(v & 0x7f) if v & 0x80 else 10.0 ** -v
                    o += (ln + 3) // 4 * 4
                ifaces.append((lt, res))
            elif btype == 6:                                       # Enhanced Packet
                iid, th, tl, cap, orig = struct.unpack(e + 'IIIII', body[:20])
                lt, res = ifaces[iid] if iid < len(ifaces) else (1, 1e-6)
                out.append((((th << 32) | tl) * res, lt, body[20:20 + cap], orig))
            elif btype == 3:                                       # Simple Packet
                orig = struct.unpack(e + 'I', body[:4])[0]
                lt, res = ifaces[0] if ifaces else (1, 1e-6)
                out.append((0.0, lt, body[4:4 + min(orig, len(body) - 4)], orig))
        except (struct.error, IndexError):
            break                       # 깨진 블록 → 거기까지 읽은 패킷은 살림
        off += blen
    return out


# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 2. 패킷 디코딩
# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
def ip4(b):
    return '.'.join(str(x) for x in b[:4])


def ip6(b):
    h = b[:16].hex()
    return ':'.join(h[i:i + 4] for i in range(0, 32, 4))


def mac(b):
    return ':'.join('%02x' % x for x in b[:6])


class Pkt:
    """디코딩된 패킷. 없는 필드는 None"""
    ts = 0.0; idx = 0; l2 = None; src = None; dst = None; proto = None
    ttl = None; ipid = None; frag_off = 0; mf = False; iplen = 0
    sport = None; dport = None; flags = None; seq = None; ack = None
    win = None; opts = b''; data = b''; itype = None; icode = None
    icmp_id = None; icmp_seq = None; inner = None; arp = None; eth_src = None
    flen = 0; rawb = b''; http = None; svc = None; wscale = None
    wlen = 0; rseq = None; rack = None; stream = None; eth_dst = None; ipdf = False
    retrans = False; reasm = False; arp_dup = False


def annotate_reasm(pkts):
    """IP 조각이 다 모이는 프레임 = Wireshark가 ICMP/UDP를 보여주는 프레임 → 필터용으로 합친 모습을 붙여 둠"""
    groups = {}
    for p in pkts:
        if p.l2 != 'ipv4' or not (p.mf or p.frag_off):
            continue
        k = (p.src, p.dst, p.ipid, p.proto)
        g = groups.setdefault(k, {'parts': {}, 'first': None, 'done': False})
        if g['done']:
            continue
        g['parts'][p.frag_off] = p
        if p.frag_off == 0:
            g['first'] = p
        ends = sorted(g['parts'])
        last = max(g['parts'].values(), key=lambda x: x.frag_off)
        if g['first'] is None or last.mf:
            continue
        pos, ok = 0, True
        for o in ends:
            x = g['parts'][o]
            if o > pos:
                ok = False
                break
            pos = max(pos, o + len(x.data) + (8 if o == 0 and x.proto in (1, 17) else 0) + (len(x.opts) + 20 if o == 0 and x.proto == 6 else 0))
        if not ok:
            continue
        g['done'] = True
        f = g['first']
        v = copy.copy(p)
        for a in ('sport', 'dport', 'itype', 'icode', 'icmp_id', 'icmp_seq', 'flags', 'seq', 'ack', 'win', 'opts', 'inner'):
            setattr(v, a, getattr(f, a))
        v.data = b''.join(g['parts'][o].data for o in ends)
        v.reasm = True
        p.rview = v


def annotate_arp(pkts):
    seen = {}
    for p in pkts:
        if p.arp and p.arp[2] != '0.0.0.0':
            m = seen.setdefault(p.arp[2], p.arp[1])
            if m != p.arp[1]:
                p.arp_dup = True


def annotate_tcp(pkts):
    """Wireshark처럼 tcp.stream 번호와 상대 시퀀스/ACK 번호(SYN = 0) 계산"""
    streams, nid = {}, 0
    for p in pkts:
        if p.proto != 6 or p.sport is None or p.mf or p.frag_off:
            continue
        a, b = (p.src, p.sport), (p.dst, p.dport)
        key = (a, b) if a <= b else (b, a)
        f = p.flags or 0
        st = streams.get(key)
        if st is None or (f & 0x12 == 0x02 and st['syn'] is not None and st['syn'] != p.seq):
            st = {'id': nid, 'syn': None, 'isn': {}}
            nid += 1
            streams[key] = st
        if f & 0x12 == 0x02:
            st['syn'] = p.seq
        if f & 0x02 or (a, b) not in st['isn']:
            st['isn'][(a, b)] = p.seq if f & 0x02 else (p.seq - 1) & 0xffffffff
        p.stream = st['id']
        p.rseq = (p.seq - st['isn'][(a, b)]) & 0xffffffff
        if f & 0x10:
            base = st['isn'].get((b, a))
            p.rack = ((p.ack - base) & 0xffffffff) if base is not None else 1
        else:
            p.rack = p.ack                     # ACK 플래그가 없으면 Wireshark도 원래 값 그대로
        if f & 0x02 and not p.data:          # 같은 SYN(시퀀스 같음)을 다시 보냄 = 재전송
            sk = (st['id'], a, p.seq, f & 0x12)
            if sk in st.setdefault('syns', set()):
                p.retrans = True
            st['syns'].add(sk)


ETHERTYPES = {0x0800: 'ipv4', 0x86dd: 'ipv6', 0x0806: 'arp', 0x88cc: 'LLDP'}


def l2_decode(lt, b):
    """링크 계층을 벗겨서 (종류, 나머지 바이트, 출발 MAC) 반환"""
    try:
        if lt == 1:                                    # Ethernet
            et = struct.unpack('>H', b[12:14])[0]
            off = 14
            while et in (0x8100, 0x88a8) and len(b) >= off + 4:   # VLAN
                et = struct.unpack('>H', b[off + 2:off + 4])[0]
                off += 4
            if et <= 1500:
                if b[:6] == b'\x01\x80\xc2\x00\x00\x00':
                    return 'STP', b, mac(b[6:12])
                if b[:6] == b'\x01\x00\x0c\xcc\xcc\xcc':
                    return 'CDP', b, mac(b[6:12])
                return 'L2기타', b, mac(b[6:12])
            return ETHERTYPES.get(et, 'L2(0x%04x)' % et), b[off:], mac(b[6:12])
        if lt == 104:                                  # Cisco HDLC
            et = struct.unpack('>H', b[2:4])[0]
            name = {0x8035: 'SLARP', 0x2000: 'CDP'}.get(et)
            return name or ETHERTYPES.get(et, 'HDLC(0x%04x)' % et), b[4:], None
        if lt in (101, 12, 14, 228, 229):              # Raw IP
            return ('ipv4' if b[0] >> 4 == 4 else 'ipv6'), b, None
        if lt == 113:                                  # Linux SLL
            et = struct.unpack('>H', b[14:16])[0]
            return ETHERTYPES.get(et, 'L2기타'), b[16:], None
        if lt == 276:                                  # Linux SLL2
            et = struct.unpack('>H', b[0:2])[0]
            return ETHERTYPES.get(et, 'L2기타'), b[20:], None
        if lt in (0, 108):                             # Loopback
            fam = struct.unpack('<I', b[:4])[0]
            if fam > 0xffff:
                fam = struct.unpack('>I', b[:4])[0]
            return ('ipv4' if fam == 2 else 'ipv6'), b[4:], None
        if lt in (9, 50):                              # PPP
            if b[:2] == b'\xff\x03':
                b = b[2:]
            pp = struct.unpack('>H', b[:2])[0]
            return {0x21: 'ipv4', 0x57: 'ipv6'}.get(pp, 'PPP기타'), b[2:], None
    except (struct.error, IndexError):
        pass
    return 'L2기타', b, None


def parse_inner(b):
    """ICMP 오류 메시지 안의 '원래 패킷' (IP 헤더 + 8바이트)"""
    if len(b) < 20 or b[0] >> 4 != 4:
        return None
    ihl = (b[0] & 15) * 4
    d = {'proto': b[9], 'src': ip4(b[12:16]), 'dst': ip4(b[16:20]), 'ttl': b[8],
         'sport': None, 'dport': None, 'icmp_seq': None, 'id': struct.unpack('>H', b[4:6])[0],
         'df': int(bool(b[6] & 0x40)), 'len': struct.unpack('>H', b[2:4])[0]}
    l4 = b[ihl:ihl + 8]
    if d['proto'] in (6, 17) and len(l4) >= 4:
        d['sport'], d['dport'] = struct.unpack('>HH', l4[:4])
        if d['proto'] == 17 and len(l4) >= 6:
            d['ulen'] = struct.unpack('>H', l4[4:6])[0]
            d['udata'] = b[ihl + 8:]
    elif d['proto'] == 1 and len(l4) >= 8:
        d['icmp_id'], d['icmp_seq'] = struct.unpack('>HH', l4[4:8])
        d['itype'], d['icode'] = l4[0], l4[1]
    return d


def decode_l4(p, proto, b):
    p.proto = proto
    if proto == 6 and len(b) >= 20:
        p.sport, p.dport, p.seq, p.ack, off, p.flags, p.win = struct.unpack('>HHIIBBH', b[:16])
        h = max(20, (off >> 4) * 4)
        p.opts, p.data = b[20:h], b[h:]
    elif proto == 17 and len(b) >= 8:
        p.sport, p.dport = struct.unpack('>HH', b[:4])
        p.data = b[8:]
    elif proto in (1, 58) and len(b) >= 4:
        t, c = b[0], b[1]
        if proto == 58:                       # ICMPv6 → ICMPv4 의미로 맞춤
            t = {128: 8, 129: 0, 1: 3, 3: 11}.get(t, 100 + t)
            p.proto = 1
        p.itype, p.icode = t, c
        if t in (0, 8, 13, 14) and len(b) >= 8:
            p.icmp_id, p.icmp_seq = struct.unpack('>HH', b[4:8])
            p.data = b[8:]
        elif t in (3, 4, 5, 11, 12):
            p.inner = parse_inner(b[8:])
            p.data = b[8:]
        else:
            p.data = b[4:]
    else:
        p.data = b


def decode(ts, idx, lt, raw):
    p = Pkt()
    p.ts, p.idx = ts, idx
    p.flen, p.rawb = len(raw), raw
    kind, b, p.eth_src = l2_decode(lt, raw)
    if lt == 1 and len(raw) >= 6:
        p.eth_dst = mac(raw[:6])
    p.l2 = kind
    try:
        if kind == 'arp' and len(b) >= 28:
            op = struct.unpack('>H', b[6:8])[0]
            p.arp = (op, mac(b[8:14]), ip4(b[14:18]), mac(b[18:24]), ip4(b[24:28]))
        elif kind == 'ipv4' and len(b) >= 20:
            ihl = (b[0] & 15) * 4
            tot = struct.unpack('>H', b[2:4])[0]
            if tot < ihl or tot > len(b):
                tot = len(b)
            p.ipid = struct.unpack('>H', b[4:6])[0]
            fl = struct.unpack('>H', b[6:8])[0]
            p.mf, p.frag_off = bool(fl & 0x2000), (fl & 0x1fff) * 8
            p.ipdf = bool(fl & 0x4000)
            p.ttl, p.src, p.dst, p.iplen = b[8], ip4(b[12:16]), ip4(b[16:20]), tot
            if p.frag_off > 0:            # 첫 조각이 아닌 IP 조각 → L4 헤더 없음
                p.proto, p.data = b[9], b[ihl:tot]
            else:
                decode_l4(p, b[9], b[ihl:tot])
        elif kind == 'ipv6' and len(b) >= 40:
            nh, p.ttl = b[6], b[7]
            p.src, p.dst = ip6(b[8:24]), ip6(b[24:40])
            p.iplen = 40 + struct.unpack('>H', b[4:6])[0]
            off = 40
            while nh in (0, 43, 60, 44) and off + 8 <= len(b):
                if nh == 44:
                    nh, off = b[off], off + 8
                else:
                    nh, off = b[off], off + (b[off + 1] + 1) * 8
            decode_l4(p, nh, b[off:])
    except (struct.error, IndexError):
        pass
    return p


# 공격/실습과 상관없는 배경 트래픽 (라우팅 프로토콜 등)
NOISE_PROTO = {88: 'EIGRP', 89: 'OSPF', 112: 'VRRP', 2: 'IGMP', 103: 'PIM'}
NOISE_UDP = {520: 'RIP', 1985: 'HSRP', 5353: 'mDNS', 1900: 'SSDP', 137: 'NetBIOS',
             138: 'NetBIOS', 67: 'DHCP', 68: 'DHCP', 5355: 'LLMNR', 546: 'DHCPv6',
             547: 'DHCPv6', 123: 'NTP', 3222: 'GLBP'}


def noise_name(p):
    if p.l2 not in ('ipv4', 'ipv6', 'arp'):
        return p.l2
    if p.l2 == 'arp':
        return None
    if p.proto in NOISE_PROTO:
        return NOISE_PROTO[p.proto]
    if p.proto == 17 and (p.sport in NOISE_UDP or p.dport in NOISE_UDP):
        return NOISE_UDP.get(p.dport) or NOISE_UDP.get(p.sport)
    if p.proto == 1 and p.itype is not None and p.itype >= 100:
        return 'ICMPv6(ND/RA 등)'
    return None


# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 3. TCP 연결(세션) 재구성
# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
KNOWN_PORTS = {20: 'FTP-DATA', 21: 'FTP', 22: 'SSH', 23: 'Telnet', 25: 'SMTP', 53: 'DNS',
               80: 'HTTP', 110: 'POP3', 111: 'RPC', 135: 'MSRPC', 139: 'NetBIOS',
               143: 'IMAP', 443: 'HTTPS', 445: 'SMB', 465: 'SMTPS', 587: 'SMTP',
               993: 'IMAPS', 995: 'POP3S', 1433: 'MSSQL', 1521: 'Oracle', 3306: 'MySQL',
               3389: 'RDP', 5432: 'PostgreSQL', 5601: 'Kibana', 5900: 'VNC',
               6379: 'Redis', 8080: 'HTTP-Alt', 8443: 'HTTPS-Alt', 9200: 'Elasticsearch'}


class Conn:
    def __init__(self, cli, srv, ts):
        self.cli, self.srv = cli, srv            # (ip, port)
        self.start = self.end = ts
        self.syn = self.synack = self.handshake = False
        self.client_rst_after_synack = False
        self.rst = [False, False]
        self.fin = [False, False]
        self.pkts = [0, 0]
        self.bytes = [0, 0]
        self.segs = [[], []]
        self.tl = []
        self.idxs = []                           # 이 세션의 모든 프레임 번호
        self._seen = set()
        self._stream = {}
        self.isn = None
        self.syn_win = self.syn_opts = None
        self.service = None

    def add(self, p, d):                         # d: 0=클라이언트→서버, 1=서버→클라이언트
        self.end = p.ts
        self.pkts[d] += 1
        self.idxs.append(p.idx)
        f = p.flags or 0
        if f & 0x02 and not f & 0x10 and d == 0:
            self.syn, self.isn, self.syn_win, self.syn_opts = True, p.seq, p.win, p.opts
        if f & 0x12 == 0x12 and d == 1:
            self.synack = True
        if d == 0 and f & 0x10 and not f & 0x06 and self.synack:
            self.handshake = True
        if f & 0x04:
            self.rst[d] = True
            if d == 0 and self.synack and not self.handshake:
                self.client_rst_after_synack = True
        if f & 0x01:
            self.fin[d] = True
        if p.data:
            k = (d, p.seq, len(p.data))
            if k in self._seen:
                p.retrans = True
            if k not in self._seen:
                self._seen.add(k)
                self.bytes[d] += len(p.data)
                self.segs[d].append((p.seq, p.data, p.idx))
                new = self._fresh(d, p.seq, p.data)       # 재전송으로 겹치는 부분은 시간순 목록에서 뺌
                if new:
                    self.tl.append((p.ts, d, new, p.idx))
                else:
                    p.retrans = True

    def _fresh(self, d, seq, data):
        """이미 받은 바이트 범위를 빼고 새 바이트만 돌려줌"""
        cov = self.__dict__.setdefault('_cov', [None, None])
        if cov[d] is None:
            cov[d] = (seq, [])
        base, iv = cov[d]
        r = (seq - base) & 0xffffffff
        if r > (1 << 31):
            r -= 1 << 32
        e = r + len(data)
        out, pos = bytearray(), r
        for a, b in iv:
            if b <= pos or a >= e:
                continue
            if a > pos:
                out += data[pos - r:a - r]
            pos = max(pos, b)
        if pos < e:
            out += data[pos - r:]
        # 범위 합치기
        iv.append((r, e))
        iv.sort()
        merged = []
        for a, b in iv:
            if merged and a <= merged[-1][1]:
                merged[-1] = (merged[-1][0], max(merged[-1][1], b))
            else:
                merged.append((a, b))
        cov[d] = (base, merged)
        return bytes(out)

    @property
    def closed(self):
        return any(self.rst) or all(self.fin)

    def stream(self, d):
        """한 방향의 데이터를 시퀀스 번호 순서로 이어붙임 (재전송 제거)"""
        if d in self._stream:
            return self._stream[d]
        segs = self.segs[d]
        if not segs:
            self._stream[d] = b''
            return b''
        base = segs[0][0]

        def rel(s):
            r = (s - base) & 0xffffffff
            return r - (1 << 32) if r > (1 << 31) else r
        out, nxt, offs = bytearray(), None, []
        for r, data, ix in sorted(((rel(s), dd, ix) for s, dd, ix in segs), key=lambda x: x[0]):
            before = len(out)
            if nxt is None or r >= nxt:
                out += data
                nxt = r + len(data)
            elif r + len(data) > nxt:
                out += data[nxt - r:]
                nxt = r + len(data)
            if len(out) > before:
                offs.append((before, len(out), ix))
        self._stream[d] = bytes(out)
        self._offs = getattr(self, '_offs', {})
        self._offs[d] = offs
        return self._stream[d]

    def frame_at(self, d, off):
        """스트림 위치 off 바이트가 들어있는 프레임 번호"""
        self.stream(d)
        for a, b, ix in self._offs.get(d, []):
            if a <= off < b:
                return ix
        return self.idxs[-1] if self.idxs else 0

    def timeline(self):
        return self.tl


def build_conns(pkts):
    conns, active = [], {}
    for p in pkts:
        if p.proto != 6 or p.sport is None:
            continue
        a, b = (p.src, p.sport), (p.dst, p.dport)
        key = (a, b) if a < b else (b, a)
        c = active.get(key)
        f = p.flags or 0
        syn_only = f & 0x02 and not f & 0x10
        if c is not None and syn_only and (p.src, p.sport) == c.cli:
            if c.closed or (c.isn is not None and p.seq != c.isn and c.handshake):
                c = None                       # 같은 포트쌍의 새 연결
        if c is None:
            if syn_only:
                cli, srv = a, b
            elif f & 0x12 == 0x12:
                cli, srv = b, a
            elif b[1] in KNOWN_PORTS and a[1] not in KNOWN_PORTS:
                cli, srv = a, b
            elif a[1] in KNOWN_PORTS and b[1] not in KNOWN_PORTS:
                cli, srv = b, a
            else:
                cli, srv = (a, b) if a[1] > b[1] else (b, a)
            c = Conn(cli, srv, p.ts)
            active[key] = c
            conns.append(c)
        c.add(p, 0 if (p.src, p.sport) == c.cli else 1)
    return conns


def _ws_opt(opts):
    i = 0
    while i < len(opts):
        k = opts[i]
        if k == 0:
            break
        if k == 1:
            i += 1
            continue
        if i + 1 >= len(opts) or opts[i + 1] < 2:
            break
        if k == 3 and opts[i + 1] == 3 and i + 2 < len(opts):
            return min(opts[i + 2], 14)
        i += opts[i + 1]
    return None


def set_window_scale(conns, pkts):
    """Wireshark의 tcp.window_size(스케일 적용값)를 맞추기 위해 SYN/SYN-ACK의 Window Scale 옵션 반영"""
    for c in conns:
        if not (c.syn and c.synack):
            continue
        syn = next((pkts[i - 1] for i in c.idxs if pkts[i - 1].flags is not None and pkts[i - 1].flags & 0x12 == 0x02), None)
        sa = next((pkts[i - 1] for i in c.idxs if pkts[i - 1].flags is not None and pkts[i - 1].flags & 0x12 == 0x12), None)
        if not syn or not sa:
            continue
        cw, sw = _ws_opt(syn.opts), _ws_opt(sa.opts)
        if cw is None or sw is None:
            continue
        for i in c.idxs:
            p = pkts[i - 1]
            if p.flags is not None and not p.flags & 0x02:
                p.wscale = cw if (p.src, p.sport) == c.cli else sw


def max_concurrency(conns):
    ev = []
    for c in conns:
        ev.append((c.start, 1))
        ev.append((c.end, -1))
    cur = best = 0
    for _, v in sorted(ev, key=lambda x: (x[0], -x[1])):
        cur += v
        best = max(best, cur)
    return best


def identify_service(c):
    cs, ss, port = c.stream(0)[:300], c.stream(1)[:300], c.srv[1]
    if cs.startswith(b'SSH-') or ss.startswith(b'SSH-'):
        return 'SSH'
    if REQ_RE.match(cs) or ss.startswith(b'HTTP/1.'):
        return 'HTTP'
    if cs[:1] == b'\x16' and cs[1:2] == b'\x03':
        return 'TLS(HTTPS 등)'
    if ss.startswith(b'+OK') or port == 110:
        return 'POP3'
    if ss.startswith(b'* OK') or port == 143:
        return 'IMAP'
    if ss.startswith(b'220') and (re.search(rb'(?i)smtp|esmtp|postfix|sendmail|exim', ss)
                                  or re.match(rb'(?i)(EHLO|HELO)', cs)):
        return 'SMTP'
    if (ss.startswith(b'220') and re.search(rb'(?i)ftp', ss)) or re.match(rb'(?i)USER ', cs) or port == 21:
        return 'FTP'
    if port == 23 or cs[:1] == b'\xff' or ss[:1] == b'\xff':
        return 'Telnet'
    return KNOWN_PORTS.get(port, 'TCP/%d' % port)


# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 4. 응용 프로토콜 분석기
# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
def lines_in_time(c):
    """(시간, 방향, 한 줄, 프레임번호) 를 시간순으로 생성"""
    buf = [b'', b'']
    for ts, d, data, ix in c.timeline():
        buf[d] += data
        while b'\n' in buf[d]:
            raw, buf[d] = buf[d].split(b'\n', 1)
            yield ts, d, raw.rstrip(b'\r').decode('utf-8', 'replace'), ix
    for d in (0, 1):
        if buf[d].strip():
            yield c.end, d, buf[d].rstrip(b'\r\n').decode('utf-8', 'replace'), (c.idxs[-1] if c.idxs else 0)


def new_attempt(user, ts, c):
    return {'user': user, 'pass': None, 'result': None, 'msg': '', 'ts': ts, 'conn': c, 'ix': []}


def analyze_ftp(c):
    r = {'banner': '', 'attempts': [], 'cmds': [], 'data_ports': set()}
    cur, last_user = None, None
    for ts, d, line, ix in lines_in_time(c):
        if d == 0:
            verb, _, arg = line.partition(' ')
            verb = verb.upper()
            if verb == 'USER':
                last_user = arg
                cur = new_attempt(arg, ts, c)
                r['attempts'].append(cur)
                cur['ix'].append(ix)
            elif verb == 'PASS':
                if cur is None or cur['pass'] is not None:
                    cur = new_attempt(last_user, ts, c)
                    r['attempts'].append(cur)
                cur['pass'] = arg
                cur['ix'].append(ix)
            else:
                r['cmds'].append([ts, line, ''])
                if verb == 'PORT':
                    n = arg.split(',')
                    if len(n) == 6 and all(x.strip().isdigit() for x in n):
                        r['data_ports'].add(int(n[4]) * 256 + int(n[5]))
                elif verb == 'EPRT' and arg:
                    parts = arg.split(arg[0])
                    if len(parts) > 3 and parts[3].isdigit():
                        r['data_ports'].add(int(parts[3]))
        else:
            m = re.match(r'(\d{3})([ -]?)(.*)', line)
            if not m:
                continue
            code, cont, text = m.groups()
            if code == '220' and not r['banner']:
                r['banner'] = text.strip()
            if cont == '-':
                continue
            if code == '227':
                mm = re.search(r'(\d+),(\d+),(\d+),(\d+),(\d+),(\d+)', line)
                if mm:
                    r['data_ports'].add(int(mm.group(5)) * 256 + int(mm.group(6)))
            if code == '229':
                mm = re.search(r'\(\|\|\|(\d+)\|\)', line)
                if mm:
                    r['data_ports'].add(int(mm.group(1)))
            if cur is not None and cur['result'] is None and code in ('230', '530', '430', '421', '500', '501', '503', '331', '332'):
                cur['ix'].append(ix)
                if code == '230':
                    cur['result'], cur['msg'] = '성공', line
                elif code not in ('331', '332'):
                    cur['result'], cur['msg'] = '실패', line
            elif r['cmds'] and not r['cmds'][-1][2]:
                r['cmds'][-1][2] = line
    return r


def strip_iac(b):
    out, i, n = bytearray(), 0, len(b)
    while i < n:
        x = b[i]
        if x == 255 and i + 1 < n:
            cmd = b[i + 1]
            if cmd == 255:
                out.append(255)
                i += 2
            elif cmd == 250:
                j = b.find(b'\xff\xf0', i + 2)
                i = n if j < 0 else j + 2
            elif cmd in (251, 252, 253, 254):
                i += 3
            else:
                i += 2
        else:
            out.append(x)
            i += 1
    return bytes(out)


def analyze_telnet(c):
    r = {'attempts': [], 'commands': [], 'banner': '', 'server_text': ''}
    mode, field, tail, cur, after, logged_in, cmd = None, '', '', None, '', False, ''
    for ts, d, data, ix in c.timeline():
        t = strip_iac(data).decode('utf-8', 'replace')
        if not t:
            continue
        if d == 1:
            r['server_text'] += t
            if not r['banner'] and t.strip():
                r['banner'] = t.strip().splitlines()[0][:80]
            tail = (tail + t)[-200:]
            if cur is not None and cur['result'] is None and cur['pass'] is not None:
                after += t
                low = after.lower()
                head = low.lstrip()[:200]
                if re.search(r'login incorrect|incorrect (login|password)|authentication fail|access denied|login invalid|'
                             r'bad password|invalid password|wrong password|password incorrect|permission denied|틀렸|잘못', head) \
                        or re.search(r'(login|username|user name)\s*:\s*$', low):
                    cur['result'], cur['msg'] = '실패', ' '.join(after.split())[:60]
                    cur['ix'].append(ix)
                elif re.search(r'last login|welcome|[$#%>]\s*$', after.rstrip('\r\n\x00 ') + ' ', re.I) or \
                        re.search(r'[$#%>]\s*$', after.rstrip('\r\n\x00')):
                    cur['result'], cur['msg'] = '성공', ' '.join(after.split())[:60]
                    cur['ix'].append(ix)
                    logged_in = True
            lt = tail.lower().rstrip()
            if re.search(r'(login|username|user name|user)\s*:\s*$', lt):
                mode, field = 'user', ''
            elif re.search(r'password\s*:\s*$', lt):
                mode, field = 'pass', ''
        else:
            for ch in t:
                if ch in '\r\n\x00':
                    if mode == 'user' and field:
                        cur = new_attempt(field, ts, c)
                        cur['ix'].append(ix)
                        r['attempts'].append(cur)
                        mode, field = None, ''
                    elif mode == 'pass':
                        if cur is None or cur['pass'] is not None:
                            cur = new_attempt(None, ts, c)
                            r['attempts'].append(cur)
                        cur['ix'].append(ix)
                        cur['pass'], after, mode, field = field, '', None, ''
                    elif logged_in and cmd.strip():
                        r['commands'].append(cmd.strip())
                        cmd = ''
                elif ch in '\x7f\x08':
                    if mode:
                        field = field[:-1]
                    else:
                        cmd = cmd[:-1]
                elif mode:
                    field += ch
                elif logged_in:
                    cmd += ch
    for a in r['attempts']:
        if a['result'] is None and a['pass'] is not None:
            a['result'] = '판단불가'
    return r


def sasl_plain(v):
    """AUTH PLAIN base64(authzid NUL user NUL pass) → (user, pass)"""
    try:
        z = base64.b64decode(v.strip() + '===')
    except Exception:
        return None
    z = z.split(b'\x00')
    if len(z) >= 3:
        return z[1].decode('utf-8', 'replace'), z[2].decode('utf-8', 'replace')
    return None


_IMAP_ARG = re.compile(r'\s*(?:"((?:[^"\\]|\\.)*)"|(\S+))')


def imap_args(t):
    out, pos = [], 0
    while pos < len(t):
        m = _IMAP_ARG.match(t, pos)
        if not m or m.end() == pos:
            break
        out.append(m.group(1).replace('\\"', '"').replace('\\\\', '\\') if m.group(1) is not None else m.group(2))
        pos = m.end()
    return out


def analyze_mail(c, svc):
    """POP3 / IMAP / SMTP 로그인 시도 (USER/PASS, LOGIN, AUTH PLAIN, AUTH LOGIN)"""
    r = {'attempts': [], 'cmds': [], 'banner': ''}
    cur, last_user, state, imap_tags, tag = None, None, None, {}, None

    def start(user, pw, ts, ix):
        a = new_attempt(user, ts, c)
        a['pass'] = pw
        a['ix'].append(ix)
        r['attempts'].append(a)
        return a
    for ts, d, line, ix in lines_in_time(c):
        if d == 1 and not r['banner']:
            r['banner'] = line[:80]
        if d == 0:
            up = line.upper()
            if svc == 'IMAP':
                parts = line.split(None, 2)
                t0 = parts[0] if parts else ''
                verb = parts[1].upper() if len(parts) > 1 else ''
                rest = parts[2] if len(parts) > 2 else ''
            else:
                t0, verb, rest = None, (line.split() or [''])[0].upper(), line.partition(' ')[2]
            # SASL 이어서 오는 줄
            if state == 'plain':
                up_ = sasl_plain(line)
                state = None
                if up_:
                    cur = start(up_[0], up_[1], ts, ix)
                    if tag:
                        imap_tags[tag] = cur
                continue
            if state == 'login_user':
                cur = start(b64(line), None, ts, ix)
                if tag:
                    imap_tags[tag] = cur
                state = 'login_pass'
                continue
            if state == 'login_pass':
                if cur is not None:
                    cur['pass'] = b64(line)
                    cur['ix'].append(ix)
                state = None
                continue
            if verb in ('AUTH', 'AUTHENTICATE'):
                mech, _, init = rest.strip().partition(' ')
                tag = t0
                if mech.upper() == 'PLAIN':
                    if init.strip() and init.strip() != '=':
                        up_ = sasl_plain(init)
                        if up_:
                            cur = start(up_[0], up_[1], ts, ix)
                            if tag:
                                imap_tags[tag] = cur
                    else:
                        state = 'plain'
                elif mech.upper() == 'LOGIN':
                    if init.strip():
                        cur = start(b64(init), None, ts, ix)
                        if tag:
                            imap_tags[tag] = cur
                        state = 'login_pass'
                    else:
                        state = 'login_user'
                continue
            if svc == 'POP3' and verb == 'USER':
                last_user = rest
                cur = start(rest, None, ts, ix)
                continue
            if svc == 'POP3' and verb == 'PASS':
                if cur is None or cur['pass'] is not None:
                    cur = start(last_user, None, ts, ix)
                cur['pass'] = rest
                cur['ix'].append(ix)
                continue
            if svc == 'IMAP' and verb == 'LOGIN':
                args = imap_args(rest)
                if len(args) >= 2:
                    cur = start(args[0], args[1], ts, ix)
                    imap_tags[t0] = cur
                    continue
            r['cmds'].append(line)
            continue
        # 서버 응답
        if svc == 'IMAP':
            m = re.match(r'(\S+)\s+(OK|NO|BAD)', line, re.I)
            if m and m.group(1) in imap_tags and imap_tags[m.group(1)]['result'] is None:
                a = imap_tags[m.group(1)]
                a['result'] = '성공' if m.group(2).upper() == 'OK' else '실패'
                a['msg'] = line
                a['ix'].append(ix)
            continue
        if cur is None or cur['pass'] is None or cur['result'] is not None:
            continue
        if svc == 'POP3':
            if line.startswith('+OK'):
                cur['result'], cur['msg'] = '성공', line
                cur['ix'].append(ix)
            elif line.startswith('-ERR'):
                cur['result'], cur['msg'] = '실패', line
                cur['ix'].append(ix)
        else:
            if line.startswith('235'):
                cur['result'], cur['msg'] = '성공', line
                cur['ix'].append(ix)
            elif line[:3] in ('535', '534', '454', '501', '500'):
                cur['result'], cur['msg'] = '실패', line
                cur['ix'].append(ix)
    for a in r['attempts']:
        if a['result'] is None and a['pass'] is not None:
            a['result'] = '응답없음'
    return r


def b64(s):
    try:
        return base64.b64decode(s.strip()).decode('utf-8', 'replace')
    except Exception:
        return s


# ── HTTP ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
HTTP_METHODS = rb'GET|POST|HEAD|PUT|DELETE|OPTIONS|PATCH|CONNECT|TRACE|PROPFIND|PROPPATCH|MKCOL|COPY|MOVE|LOCK|UNLOCK|SEARCH|REPORT|CHECKOUT|CHECKIN|MERGE|MKACTIVITY|MKWORKSPACE|VERSION-CONTROL|BASELINE-CONTROL|LABEL|UPDATE|ACL|SUBSCRIBE|UNSUBSCRIBE|NOTIFY|POLL|M-SEARCH|MKCALENDAR|BPROPFIND|BMOVE|BDELETE|BCOPY|RPC_IN_DATA|RPC_OUT_DATA'
REQ_RE = re.compile(rb'(' + HTTP_METHODS + rb'|[A-Z][A-Z_\-]{2,15}) ([^\r\n]+?) HTTP/1\.[01]\r?\n')
RESP_RE = re.compile(rb'HTTP/1\.[01] (\d{3})([^\r\n]*)\r?\n')
WS_METHODS = set(HTTP_METHODS.decode().split('|'))


def dechunk(s, i):
    out = bytearray()
    while i < len(s):
        j = s.find(b'\r\n', i)
        if j < 0:
            break
        try:
            n = int(s[i:j].split(b';')[0].strip() or b'0', 16)
        except ValueError:
            break
        i = j + 2
        if n == 0:
            k = s.find(b'\r\n\r\n', i - 2)
            return bytes(out), (k + 4 if k >= 0 else i)
        out += s[i:i + n]
        i += n + 2
    return bytes(out), i


def decode_body(body, hdr):
    enc = hdr.get('content-encoding', '').lower()
    try:
        if 'gzip' in enc or body[:2] == b'\x1f\x8b':
            return zlib.decompressobj(16 + zlib.MAX_WBITS).decompress(body)
        if 'deflate' in enc:
            try:
                return zlib.decompress(body)
            except zlib.error:
                return zlib.decompressobj(-zlib.MAX_WBITS).decompress(body)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return body


def parse_http(stream, is_req):
    rx = REQ_RE if is_req else RESP_RE
    msgs, pos = [], 0
    while True:
        m = rx.search(stream, pos)
        if not m:
            break
        he = stream.find(b'\r\n\r\n', m.start())
        head = stream[m.start():he if he >= 0 else len(stream)]
        bstart = he + 4 if he >= 0 else len(stream)
        lines = head.decode('latin-1').split('\r\n')
        hdr = {}
        for l in lines[1:]:
            k, _, v = l.partition(':')
            hdr[k.strip().lower()] = v.strip()
        nxt = bstart
        mstart = m.start()
        if 'chunked' in hdr.get('transfer-encoding', '').lower():
            body, nxt = dechunk(stream, bstart)
        elif hdr.get('content-length', '').isdigit():
            cl = int(hdr['content-length'])
            body, nxt = stream[bstart:bstart + cl], bstart + cl
        elif not is_req:
            n2 = rx.search(stream, bstart)
            nxt = n2.start() if n2 else len(stream)
            body = stream[bstart:nxt]
        else:
            body = b''
        msg = {'first': lines[0], 'hdr': hdr, 'raw_len': len(body), 'body': decode_body(body, hdr),
               'off': mstart, 'end': max(nxt, bstart), 'hdr_end': bstart}
        if is_req:
            msg['method'], msg['uri'] = m.group(1).decode(), m.group(2).decode('latin-1')
        else:
            msg['code'], msg['reason'] = int(m.group(1)), m.group(2).decode('latin-1').strip()
        msgs.append(msg)
        pos = max(nxt, m.end())
    return msgs


def analyze_http(c):
    if getattr(c, '_http', None) is not None:
        return c._http
    reqs, allr = parse_http(c.stream(0), True), parse_http(c.stream(1), False)
    resps = [x for x in allr if not 100 <= x['code'] < 200]      # 100 Continue 등은 짝짓기에서 뺌
    for i, q in enumerate(reqs):
        if i < len(resps) and (q['method'] == 'HEAD' or resps[i]['code'] in (204, 304)):
            resps[i]['end'], resps[i]['body'] = resps[i]['hdr_end'], b''
    for d, msgs in ((0, reqs), (1, allr)):
        for m in msgs:                      # Wireshark처럼 메시지가 끝나는 프레임에 HTTP 표시
            m['frame'] = c.frame_at(d, max(m['off'], m['end'] - 1))
    pairs = []
    for i, q in enumerate(reqs):
        pairs.append((q, resps[i] if i < len(resps) else None))
    c._http = pairs
    c._http_all = reqs + allr
    return pairs


def http_title(body):
    m = re.search(rb'(?is)<title[^>]*>(.*?)</title>', body or b'')
    if m:
        return re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', m.group(1).decode('utf-8', 'replace')).strip()[:80]
    return ''


LOGIN_KEYS = re.compile(r'(?i)^(user(name)?|id|uid|login|userid|user_id|email|account|name|log|usr|mb_id)$')
PASS_KEYS = re.compile(r'(?i)(pass|pwd|pw|password|passwd|mb_password)')


def http_login_result(r, q=None):
    """HTTP 로그인 응답으로 성공/실패 추정"""
    if not r:
        return '응답없음'
    code = r['code']
    body = (r['body'] or b'')[:8000].decode('utf-8', 'replace').lower()
    t = http_title(r['body']).lower()
    text = body + ' ' + t
    loc = r['hdr'].get('location', '')
    if code in (401, 403, 407):
        return '실패(%d)' % code
    if code in (301, 302, 303, 307, 308):
        if re.search(r'(?i)log_?in|sign_?in|logon|auth|fail|error|denied|invalid|retry|again', loc):
            return '실패 추정(로그인 페이지로 되돌아감 → %s)' % loc
        return '성공 추정(리다이렉트 → %s)' % loc
    if re.search(r'welcome|dashboard|logout|log out|sign out|signout|login success|logged in|환영|로그아웃|로그인 성공|관리자 페이지|마이페이지', text):
        return '성공 추정(응답에 로그인 후 문구)'
    if re.search(r'login fail|fail|incorrect|invalid|wrong|denied|not match|try again|실패|틀렸|잘못|일치하지|없는 계정|다시 시도', text):
        return '실패(응답에 실패 문구)'
    if q is not None and q['hdr'].get('authorization', '').lower().startswith('basic ') and 200 <= code < 300:
        return '성공(Basic 인증 통과 %d)' % code
    return '판단불가(%d)' % code


def http_creds(q):
    """요청에서 계정/비밀번호 추출 (Basic 인증, POST 폼, GET 파라미터)"""
    out = []
    auth = q['hdr'].get('authorization', '')
    if auth.lower().startswith('basic '):
        dec = b64(auth[6:])
        u, _, pw = dec.partition(':')
        out.append(('Basic 인증', u, pw))
    params = {}
    body = q['body'] or b''
    if body:
        try:
            params.update(urllib.parse.parse_qs(body.decode('utf-8', 'replace'), keep_blank_values=True))
        except Exception:
            pass
    if '?' in q['uri']:
        params.update(urllib.parse.parse_qs(q['uri'].split('?', 1)[1], keep_blank_values=True))
    u = next((v[0] for k, v in params.items() if LOGIN_KEYS.match(k)), None)
    pw = next((v[0] for k, v in params.items() if PASS_KEYS.search(k)), None)
    if pw is not None:
        out.append(('폼 파라미터', u, pw))
    return out


# ── DNS ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
QTYPES = {1: 'A', 2: 'NS', 5: 'CNAME', 6: 'SOA', 12: 'PTR', 15: 'MX', 16: 'TXT',
          28: 'AAAA', 33: 'SRV', 255: 'ANY', 65: 'HTTPS', 64: 'SVCB'}
RCODES = {0: '정상', 1: '형식오류', 2: '서버실패', 3: '없는 도메인(NXDOMAIN)', 4: '미구현', 5: '거부'}


def dns_name(b, off):
    labels, end, hops = [], None, 0
    while off < len(b):
        l = b[off]
        if l == 0:
            off += 1
            break
        if l & 0xC0 == 0xC0:
            if off + 1 >= len(b) or hops > 15:
                break
            if end is None:
                end = off + 2
            off, hops = ((l & 0x3f) << 8) | b[off + 1], hops + 1
            continue
        labels.append(b[off + 1:off + 1 + l].decode('latin-1'))
        off += 1 + l
    return ('.'.join(labels) or '.'), (end if end is not None else off)


def parse_dns(b):
    if len(b) < 12:
        return None
    tid, fl, qd, an = struct.unpack('>HHHH', b[:8])
    msg = {'id': tid, 'qr': fl >> 15, 'rcode': fl & 15, 'q': [], 'a': []}
    off = 12
    try:
        for _ in range(min(qd, 20)):
            name, off = dns_name(b, off)
            qt = struct.unpack('>H', b[off:off + 2])[0]
            off += 4
            msg['q'].append((name, QTYPES.get(qt, str(qt))))
        for _ in range(min(an, 50)):
            name, off = dns_name(b, off)
            t, _c, _ttl, rl = struct.unpack('>HHIH', b[off:off + 10])
            off += 10
            rd = b[off:off + rl]
            if t == 1 and rl == 4:
                v = ip4(rd)
            elif t == 28 and rl == 16:
                v = ip6(rd)
            elif t in (2, 5, 12):
                v = dns_name(b, off)[0]
            elif t == 15:
                v = dns_name(b, off + 2)[0]
            elif t == 16:
                v = rd[1:].decode('latin-1')
            else:
                v = rd.hex()[:40]
            off += rl
            msg['a'].append((name, QTYPES.get(t, str(t)), v))
    except (struct.error, IndexError):
        pass
    return msg


BROWSER_DOMAINS = re.compile(r'(?i)(mozilla|firefox|google|gstatic|googleapis|msftconnecttest|msftncsi|'
                             r'microsoft|windowsupdate|bing\.com|apple\.com|icloud|openh264|'
                             r'digicert|ocsp|cloudflare|akamai|doubleclick)')

ICMP_NAMES = {0: 'Echo Reply', 3: 'Destination Unreachable', 4: 'Source Quench', 5: 'Redirect',
              8: 'Echo Request', 11: 'Time Exceeded', 12: 'Parameter Problem',
              13: 'Timestamp Request', 14: 'Timestamp Reply'}
UNREACH = {0: 'net', 1: 'host', 2: 'protocol', 3: 'port', 4: 'frag needed', 9: 'net prohibited',
           10: 'host prohibited', 13: 'admin prohibited'}


# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 5. 파일 하나 분석 → 판정(finding) 목록 만들기
# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
class Finding:
    def __init__(self, prio, title, summary, lines=None, ev=None, wf=None, groups=None):
        self.prio, self.title, self.summary, self.lines = prio, title, summary, lines or []
        self.ev = list(ev or [])     # 근거 패킷 프레임 번호 (Wireshark No. 와 같음)
        self.wf = list(wf or [])     # Wireshark 디스플레이 필터 [(필터, 설명)]
        self.groups = groups         # [(소제목, [프레임번호...]), ...] — 있으면 묶어서 표시


WF_AUTH = {'FTP': 'ftp.request.command == "USER" || ftp.request.command == "PASS" || ftp.response.code == 230 || ftp.response.code == 530',
           'Telnet': 'telnet', 'POP3': 'pop', 'IMAP': 'imap', 'SMTP': 'smtp'}
WF_PROTO = {'FTP': 'ftp', 'Telnet': 'telnet', 'POP3': 'pop', 'IMAP': 'imap', 'SMTP': 'smtp'}
STD_PORT = {'FTP': 21, 'Telnet': 23, 'POP3': 110, 'IMAP': 143, 'SMTP': 25, 'HTTP': 80, 'SSH': 22}


def fmt_ts(ts, full=True):
    try:
        return datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(ts).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S' if full else '%H:%M:%S')
    except (OverflowError, OSError, ValueError):
        return '%.3f' % ts


def ep(x):
    return '%s:%s' % x


def describe_targets(ips):
    ips = sorted(set(ips), key=lambda s: [int(x) if x.isdigit() else 0 for x in s.split('.')])
    nets = collections.Counter(i.rsplit('.', 1)[0] for i in ips if i.count('.') == 3)
    if len(nets) == 1 and len(ips) > 3:
        net = next(iter(nets))
        return '%s.0/24 대역 %d개 IP (%s ~ %s)' % (net, len(ips), ips[0], ips[-1])
    return ', '.join(ips[:8]) + (' 외 %d개' % (len(ips) - 8) if len(ips) > 8 else '')


def short_list(items, n=12):
    items = list(items)
    s = ', '.join(str(x) for x in items[:n])
    return s + (' … (총 %d개)' % len(items) if len(items) > n else '')


def ports_str(ports):
    return ', '.join('%d(%s)' % (p, KNOWN_PORTS[p]) if p in KNOWN_PORTS else str(p) for p in sorted(ports))


def guess_os_by_ttl(ttl):
    if ttl is None:
        return ''
    if ttl <= 64:
        return 'Linux/Unix 계열(초기 TTL 64)'
    if ttl <= 128:
        return 'Windows 계열(초기 TTL 128)'
    return '네트워크 장비/Solaris 등(초기 TTL 255)'


def _safe(fn, *a):
    """탐지기 하나가 실패해도 나머지 분석은 살림"""
    try:
        return fn(*a)
    except Exception as e:
        return [Finding(97, '일부 분석 실패', '%s: %s: %s' % (fn.__name__, type(e).__name__, e),
                        ['손상된 패킷 등으로 이 항목만 분석하지 못했습니다. 다른 결과는 정상입니다.'])]


def analyze_file(path, verbose=False):
    raw = read_capture(path)
    pkts = [decode(ts, i + 1, lt, b) for i, (ts, lt, b, _o) in enumerate(raw)]
    for p, x in zip(pkts, raw):
        p.wlen = max(x[3] or 0, p.flen)
    annotate_tcp(pkts)
    annotate_reasm(pkts)
    annotate_arp(pkts)
    info = {'path': path, 'count': len(pkts), 'linktypes': sorted({x[1] for x in raw}),
            'start': min((p.ts for p in pkts), default=0), 'end': max((p.ts for p in pkts), default=0)}
    noise, main = collections.Counter(), []
    for p in pkts:
        n = noise_name(p)
        if n:
            noise[n] += 1
        else:
            main.append(p)
    info['noise'] = noise
    findings = []
    conns = build_conns(main)
    set_window_scale(conns, pkts)
    for c in conns:
        if c.bytes[0] or c.bytes[1]:
            c.service = identify_service(c)
            for ix in c.idxs:
                pkts[ix - 1].svc = c.service
            if c.service == 'HTTP':
                analyze_http(c)
                for m in c._http_all:
                    if 'method' in m and m['method'] not in WS_METHODS:
                        continue                 # Wireshark가 HTTP로 안 보는 이상한 메서드(nmap 프로브 등)
                    if 1 <= m['frame'] <= len(pkts):
                        pkts[m['frame'] - 1].http = m

    # ── (A) 응용 계층 세션 (FTP/Telnet/HTTP/SSH/메일) + 무차별 대입 판단 ─────────
    groups = collections.OrderedDict()
    for c in conns:
        if c.service:
            groups.setdefault((c.cli[0], c.srv[0], c.srv[1], c.service), []).append(c)
    ftp_data_ports = set()
    used_conns = set()
    creds = []
    for (cip, sip, sport, svc), cs in groups.items():
        if svc in ('FTP', 'Telnet', 'POP3', 'IMAP', 'SMTP'):
            if svc == 'FTP':
                res = [analyze_ftp(c) for c in cs]
                for r in res:
                    ftp_data_ports |= r['data_ports']
            elif svc == 'Telnet':
                res = [analyze_telnet(c) for c in cs]
            else:
                res = [analyze_mail(c, svc) for c in cs]
            fg = _safe(auth_findings, svc, cip, sip, sport, cs, res, verbose)
            findings += fg
            creds += collect_auth_creds(svc, cip, sip, sport, res, any('무차별' in x.title for x in fg))
        elif svc == 'HTTP':
            fg = _safe(http_findings, cip, sip, sport, cs, verbose)
            findings += fg
            creds += collect_http_creds(cip, sip, sport, cs, any('무차별' in x.title for x in fg))
        elif svc == 'SSH':
            findings += _safe(ssh_findings, cip, sip, sport, cs, verbose)
        else:
            continue
        used_conns.update(id(c) for c in cs)

    # FTP 데이터 채널 (파일 내용/목록)
    for c in conns:
        if (c.bytes[0] or c.bytes[1]) and (c.srv[1] in ftp_data_ports or c.cli[1] in ftp_data_ports or c.srv[1] == 20 or c.cli[1] == 20):
            used_conns.add(id(c))
            for ix in c.idxs:
                pkts[ix - 1].svc = 'FTP-DATA'
            d = 1 if c.bytes[1] >= c.bytes[0] else 0
            data = c.stream(d)
            findings.append(Finding(52, 'FTP 데이터 전송', 'FTP 데이터 채널 %d바이트' % len(data),
                                    ['%s → %s  %d바이트' % (ep(c.cli), ep(c.srv), len(data)),
                                     '내용 미리보기: ' + preview(data, 300 if verbose else 150)],
            ev=c.idxs, wf=[('ftp-data', 'FTP 데이터 채널(파일 내용/목록)')]))

    # 기타 TCP 데이터 세션
    other = [c for c in conns if c.service and id(c) not in used_conns and c.service != 'TLS(HTTPS 등)'
             and 4444 not in (c.srv[1], c.cli[1]) and not SHELL_RX.search(c.stream(0)[:20000] + c.stream(1)[:20000])]
    if other:
        lines = []
        for c in other[:30]:
            first = c.stream(0)[:60] or c.stream(1)[:60]
            lines.append('%s → %s [%s] 송신 %dB / 수신 %dB  %s' % (ep(c.cli), ep(c.srv), c.service,
                                                                 c.bytes[0], c.bytes[1], preview(first, 60)))
        findings.append(Finding(58, '기타 TCP 통신', '%d개 세션 (%s)' % (len(other), ', '.join(sorted({c.service for c in other}))), lines,
            ev=[i for c in other[:10] for i in c.idxs[:6]], wf=[('tcp.port == %d' % other[0].srv[1], '첫 세션 서버 포트')]))

    # ── (B) 포트 스캔 ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    scan_pairs = collections.defaultdict(list)
    for c in conns:
        if c.syn and not (c.bytes[0] or c.bytes[1]) or (c.syn and c.service is None):
            scan_pairs[(c.cli[0], c.srv[0])].append(c)
    nmap_probe_srcs = nmap_discovery_signature(main)
    for (a, v), cs in scan_pairs.items():
        ports = {c.srv[1] for c in cs}
        is_nmap = any(c.syn_win in (1024, 2048, 3072, 4096) and c.syn_opts == b'\x02\x04\x05\xb4' for c in cs)
        is_hping = sum(1 for c in cs if c.syn_win == 512 and not c.syn_opts) >= len(cs) * 0.5
        if len(ports) < 6 and not (len(ports) >= 2 and (is_nmap or is_hping)):
            continue
        opened = sorted({c.srv[1] for c in cs if c.synack})
        closed = sorted({c.srv[1] for c in cs if not c.synack and c.rst[1]} - set(opened))
        filtered = sorted(ports - set(opened) - set(closed))
        half = sum(1 for c in cs if c.client_rst_after_synack)
        full = sum(1 for c in cs if c.handshake)
        os_opts = sum(1 for c in cs if c.syn_opts and len(c.syn_opts) >= 12) >= len(cs) * 0.5
        if half == full == 0 and not is_nmap and os_opts:
            tech = 'TCP Connect 스캔(nmap -sT 추정) — 열린 포트 없음, SYN 옵션이 OS 기본값(connect() 사용)'
        elif half >= full:
            tech = 'TCP SYN 스캔(하프오픈, nmap -sS) — 열린 포트의 SYN/ACK에 공격자가 RST로 끊음'
        else:
            tech = 'TCP Connect 스캔(nmap -sT) — 3-way 핸드셰이크 완료 후 종료'
        lines = ['공격자 %s → 대상 %s' % (a, v),
                 '기간 %s ~ %s' % (fmt_ts(min(c.start for c in cs)), fmt_ts(max(c.end for c in cs), False)),
                 '스캔 방식: ' + tech,
                 '검사한 포트 %d개: %s' % (len(ports), ports_str(ports) if len(ports) <= 40 else short_list(sorted(ports), 40)),
                 '▶ 열린 포트(SYN/ACK 응답): ' + (ports_str(opened) or '없음'),
                 '  닫힌 포트(RST 응답): %d개 %s' % (len(closed), ports_str(closed) if len(closed) <= 30 else ''),
                 '  필터링/무응답: %d개 %s' % (len(filtered), ports_str(filtered) if len(filtered) <= 30 else '')]
        if is_nmap:
            wins = sorted({c.syn_win for c in cs if c.syn_opts == b'\x02\x04\x05\xb4'})
            lines.append('nmap 특징: SYN 윈도우 크기 %s + TCP 옵션이 MSS 하나뿐, 출발 포트 고정' % ','.join(str(x) for x in wins[:4]))
        if a in nmap_probe_srcs:
            lines.append('nmap 특징: 스캔 직전 호스트 확인 프로브(ICMP Echo, ICMP Timestamp, TCP 443 SYN, TCP 80 ACK)')
        if is_hping:
            lines.append('hping3 특징: SYN 윈도우 512 + TCP 옵션 없음 (hping3 --scan / -S)')
        if not is_nmap and not is_hping and full > half:
            lines.append('일반 OS의 connect() 사용(윈도우·옵션이 정상값) → nmap -sT 또는 nc -z 등')
        NMAP_PROBES = (b'GET / HTTP/1.0\r\n\r\n', b'OPTIONS / HTTP/1.0\r\n\r\n', b'OPTIONS / RTSP/1.0\r\n\r\n',
                       b'HELP\r\n', b'\r\n\r\n', b'HEAD / HTTP/1.0\r\n\r\n')
        svc_conns = [c for c in conns if c.cli[0] == a and c.srv[0] == v and c.service and c.bytes[1]
                     and (c.stream(0) in NMAP_PROBES or not c.bytes[0])]
        if svc_conns:
            seen_p = collections.OrderedDict()
            for c in svc_conns:
                seen_p.setdefault(c.srv[1], c)
            lines.append('▶ 서비스/버전 탐지(-sV/-A) 결과: ' + ', '.join(
                '%d:%s' % (port, (banner_of(c) or preview(c.stream(1)[:50], 50))) for port, c in list(seen_p.items())[:8]))
        tool = 'nmap ' if (is_nmap or a in nmap_probe_srcs) else 'hping3 ' if is_hping else ''
        findings.append(Finding(30, '%s포트 스캔' % tool,
                                '열린 포트 %s (%d개 검사) | %s → %s' % (','.join(map(str, opened)) or '없음', len(ports), a, v),
                                lines,
            ev=[i for c in cs if c.synack for i in c.idxs] + [i for c in [x for x in cs if not x.synack][:2] for i in c.idxs],
            groups=[('열린 포트: SYN → SYN/ACK → (공격자)RST', [i for c in cs if c.synack for i in c.idxs]),
                    ('닫힌 포트 예시: SYN → RST/ACK', [i for c in [x for x in cs if not x.synack][:3] for i in c.idxs])], wf=[('ip.src == %s && tcp.flags.syn == 1 && tcp.flags.ack == 0' % a, '공격자가 보낸 스캔 SYN'), ('ip.src == %s && tcp.flags.syn == 1 && tcp.flags.ack == 1' % v, '열린 포트의 SYN/ACK 응답')]))

    findings += _safe(stealth_scan_findings, main, conns)
    findings += _safe(udp_scan_findings, [p for p in pkts if p.src])

    # ── (C) 호스트 탐색(핑 스윕) ─────────────────────────────────────
    findings += _safe(sweep_findings, main, nmap_probe_srcs)

    # ── (D) traceroute / ping / 기타 ICMP ───────────────────────────
    trace = _safe(traceroute_findings, main)
    findings += trace
    trace_pairs = {(f.src, f.dst) for f in trace}
    findings += _safe(ping_findings, main, trace_pairs, {f_src for f_src in sweep_sources(main)})

    # ── (E) DoS / 기타 공격 ─────────────────────────────────────────
    findings += _safe(dos_findings, main, conns, sweep_sources(main))
    findings += _safe(tool_findings, main, conns, pkts)
    findings += _safe(l2_attack_findings, pkts)
    tool_titles = ' '.join(f.title for f in findings)
    if re.search(r'Nikto|sqlmap|디렉터리/파일 무차별|웹 취약점 스캔|nmap 스크립트|nmap 서비스 버전|ARTEX|웹 공격 페이로드|무차별 대입', tool_titles):
        for f in findings:
            if f.title == 'HTTP 웹 접속' and '로그인 정보' not in ' '.join(f.lines):
                f.prio = 82
    if re.search(r'nmap (서비스 버전|스크립트|OS 탐지)', tool_titles):
        for f in findings:
            if f.title in ('FTP 접속', 'SSH 접속', 'Telnet 접속', '기타 TCP 통신') and '로그인:' not in ' '.join(f.lines):
                f.prio = 83
    hp = [f for f in findings if f.title.startswith('hping3 사용 흔적')]
    if hp:
        hosts = [f for f in findings if re.search('SYN Flooding|Land 공격|hping3 포트 스캔', f.title)]
        for f in hosts:
            f.title = f.title + ' — hping3'
            f.lines += [l for l in hp[0].lines if l.lstrip().startswith(('▶ 도구', '근거'))]
        if hosts:
            findings = [f for f in findings if f not in hp]
    findings += _safe(arp_findings, main)

    # ── (F) DNS ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    findings += _safe(dns_findings, main, bool(findings), verbose)

    if not findings:
        findings.append(Finding(99, '분류 불가', '알려진 패턴 없음 — 3 전체 패킷 탭에서 Enter로 패킷 해부해서 확인',
                                flow_summary(main),
            ev=[p.idx for p in main[:40]]))
    findings.sort(key=lambda f: f.prio)
    info['flows'] = flow_summary(main)
    info['pkts'], info['raw'], info['conns'], info['creds'] = pkts, raw, conns, creds
    return info, findings


def preview(b, n=80):
    s = b[:n].decode('utf-8', 'replace')
    s = ''.join(ch if (ch.isprintable() or ch == ' ') else ('↵' if ch == '\n' else '.') for ch in s.replace('\r', ''))
    return s + ('…' if len(b) > n else '')

def _http_reqs(conns):
    out = []
    for c in conns:
        if c.service == 'HTTP':
            out += [(c, q, r) for q, r in analyze_http(c)]
    return out


def req_url(c, q):
    if q['uri'].startswith(('http://', 'https://')):
        return q['uri']
    return 'http://%s%s' % (q['hdr'].get('host', c.srv[0]), q['uri'])


def _unq(s):
    try:
        return urllib.parse.unquote_plus(urllib.parse.unquote_plus(s))
    except Exception:
        return s


def ssh_auth(c):
    """암호화된 SSH 세션에서 인증 결과 추정.
    키 교환 후 서버의 첫 암호화 패킷 = SERVICE_ACCEPT(크기 A).
    인증 실패(USERAUTH_FAILURE)는 A보다 크고, 성공(USERAUTH_SUCCESS)은 A보다 작다 (실측 검증)."""
    s = c.stream(1)
    nl = s.find(b'\n')
    if not s.startswith(b'SSH-') or nl < 0:
        return None
    pos, enc = nl + 1, None
    while pos + 6 <= len(s):
        plen = struct.unpack('>I', s[pos:pos + 4])[0]
        if plen < 5 or plen > 70000:
            break
        msg = s[pos + 5]
        pos += 4 + plen
        if msg == 21:            # SSH_MSG_NEWKEYS
            enc = pos
            break
    if enc is None:
        return {'stage': '키 교환 미완료', 'success': None}
    off, segs, prev_dir = 0, [], None
    for ts, d, data, ix in c.timeline():
        if d == 1:
            if off >= enc:
                segs.append((len(data), ix, prev_dir))
            off += len(data)
        prev_dir = d
    if not segs:
        return {'stage': '인증 전 종료', 'success': None}
    a = segs[0][0]
    later = segs[1:]
    succ = next(((n, ix) for n, ix, pd in later if n < a and pd == 0), None)
    fails = []
    for n, ix, pd in later:
        if succ and ix >= succ[1]:
            break
        if n > a and pd == 0 and n < 400:
            fails.append(n)
    return {'stage': '인증', 'success': bool(succ), 'succ_frame': succ[1] if succ else None, 'succ_size': succ[0] if succ else None,
            'accept': a, 'fail_size': collections.Counter(fails).most_common(1)[0][0] if fails else None, 'fails': len(fails)}


def tls_sni(c):
    b = c.stream(0)
    if len(b) < 50 or b[0] != 0x16 or b[5] != 0x01:
        return None
    try:
        p = 9 + 2 + 32
        p += 1 + b[p]
        p += 2 + struct.unpack('>H', b[p:p + 2])[0]
        p += 1 + b[p]
        end = p + 2 + struct.unpack('>H', b[p:p + 2])[0]
        p += 2
        while p + 4 <= end:
            t, l = struct.unpack('>HH', b[p:p + 4])
            if t == 0:
                return b[p + 9:p + 9 + struct.unpack('>H', b[p + 7:p + 9])[0]].decode('latin-1')
            p += 4 + l
    except (IndexError, struct.error):
        pass
    return None


SHELL_RX = re.compile(rb'(?m)uid=\d+\([\w.-]+\)\s+gid=\d+|^[\w.-]+@[\w.-]+:[^\n]{0,60}[#$] ?$|/bin/(ba)?sh: |'
                      rb"can't access tty|Microsoft Windows \[Version|[A-Z]:\\[^\n>]{0,60}>|^# $")
CMD_RX = re.compile(r'^(id|whoami|uname|ls|cat|pwd|cd|ifconfig|ip a|wget|curl|nc|python|bash|sh|echo|ps|netstat|hostname|'
                    r'dir|ipconfig|net |type |chmod|rm |cp |mv |find|sudo|su )')


def tool_findings(main, conns, pkts):
    out = []
    reqs = _http_reqs(conns)
    # ── 1) User-Agent 로 도구 식별 ──────────────────────────────
    ua_hits = collections.OrderedDict()
    for c, q, r in reqs:
        ua = q['hdr'].get('user-agent', '')
        for rx, tool, desc in UA_SIGS:
            if ua and re.search(rx, ua):
                e = ua_hits.setdefault(tool, {'desc': desc, 'ua': ua, 'n': 0, 'cli': set(), 'srv': set(), 'ev': [], 'reqs': []})
                e['n'] += 1
                e['cli'].add(c.cli[0])
                e['srv'].add(c.srv[0])
                e['reqs'].append((c, q, r))
                if len(e['ev']) < 20:
                    e['ev'].append(q.get('frame') or (c.tl[0][3] if c.tl else 0))
                break
    # ── 2) 웹 공격 페이로드 ──────────────────────────────────────
    atk = collections.OrderedDict()
    tool_atk = collections.defaultdict(collections.Counter)
    for c, q, r in reqs:
        ua = q['hdr'].get('user-agent', '')
        owner = next((t for rx, t, d in UA_SIGS if ua and re.search(rx, ua)), None)
        blob = _unq(q['uri']) + ' ' + _unq((q['body'] or b'')[:4000].decode('latin-1')) + ' ' + \
            ' '.join(v for k, v in q['hdr'].items() if k in ('user-agent', 'referer', 'cookie', 'x-api-version', 'x-forwarded-for'))
        for name, rx in WEB_ATTACKS:
            if re.search(rx, blob, re.I):
                if owner in SCANNER_TOOLS or owner in ('sqlmap', 'nmap NSE', 'Hydra') or owner in DIRBUST_TOOLS:
                    tool_atk[owner][name] += 1
                    continue
                e = atk.setdefault(name, [])
                e.append((c, q, r))
    # ── 3) 디렉터리 무차별 대입 / 스캐너 행동 ────────────────────
    by_pair = collections.defaultdict(list)
    for c, q, r in reqs:
        by_pair[(c.cli[0], c.srv[0], c.srv[1])].append((c, q, r))
    tools_by_pair = {}
    for tool, e in ua_hits.items():
        for c, q, r in e['reqs']:
            tools_by_pair.setdefault((c.cli[0], c.srv[0], c.srv[1]), set()).add(tool)

    for tool, e in ua_hits.items():
        if tool in ('Hydra', 'nmap NSE'):
            continue                                     # 무차별 대입 / nmap 세부기능 판정에서 따로 처리
        if tool in ('curl', 'wget', 'Python 스크립트', 'Go 프로그램') and not any(t.startswith('ARTEX') for t in ua_hits):
            if e['n'] < 20:
                continue                                 # 소량의 일반 도구 요청은 생략
        codes = collections.Counter(r['code'] for c, q, r in e['reqs'] if r)
        found = collections.OrderedDict()
        if tool in DIRBUST_TOOLS | SCANNER_TOOLS:
            for c, q, r in e['reqs']:
                if r and r['code'] not in (404, 400, 405, 501) and len(found) < 25:
                    found.setdefault(q['uri'].split('?')[0] if not q['uri'].startswith('http') else req_url(c, q), r['code'])
        lines = ['도구: %s — %s' % (tool, e['desc']),
                 'User-Agent: ' + e['ua'],
                 '출발지 %s → 대상 %s, 요청 %d개' % (', '.join(sorted(e['cli'])), ', '.join(sorted(e['srv'])), e['n']),
                 '응답 코드: ' + ', '.join('%s×%d' % kv for kv in codes.most_common(6))]
        prio, title = 21, '%s 사용 흔적' % tool
        if tool in SCANNER_TOOLS:
            title, prio = '%s 웹 취약점 스캔' % tool, 21
        elif tool in DIRBUST_TOOLS:
            title, prio = '%s 디렉터리/파일 무차별 대입' % tool, 21
        elif tool == 'sqlmap':
            title, prio = 'sqlmap SQL 인젝션 공격', 19
            params = collections.Counter()
            tech = set()
            vuln = False
            for c, q, r in e['reqs']:
                qs = q['uri'].split('?', 1)[1] if '?' in q['uri'] else (q['body'] or b'').decode('latin-1')
                for k in urllib.parse.parse_qs(qs, keep_blank_values=True):
                    params[k] += 1
                u = _unq(q['uri']).upper()
                if 'SLEEP(' in u or 'BENCHMARK(' in u or 'WAITFOR' in u:
                    tech.add('시간 기반(Time-based)')
                if 'UNION' in u and 'SELECT' in u:
                    tech.add('UNION 기반')
                if re.search(r'AND \d+=\d+|OR \d+=\d+', u):
                    tech.add('불리언 기반(Boolean-based)')
                if 'EXTRACTVALUE' in u or 'UPDATEXML' in u or 'FLOOR(RAND' in u:
                    tech.add('에러 기반(Error-based)')
                if r and re.search(rb'(?i)sql syntax|mysql_fetch|ORA-\d|SQLSTATE|syntax error', r['body'] or b''):
                    vuln = True
            lines.append('▶ 공격 대상 파라미터: ' + ', '.join('%s(%d회)' % kv for kv in params.most_common(4)))
            if tech:
                lines.append('  사용 기법: ' + ', '.join(sorted(tech)))
            lines.append('▶ 결과: ' + ('서버가 DB 에러 메시지를 응답 → SQL 인젝션 취약 가능성 높음' if vuln else 'DB 에러 응답은 없음'))
        elif tool.startswith('ARTEX'):
            continue                                     # ARTEX 종합 판정에서 처리
        elif tool == 'nmap NSE':
            title, prio = 'nmap 스크립트 스캔(NSE: -sC / -A)', 31
        if tool_atk.get(tool):
            lines.append('▶ 보낸 공격 테스트: ' + ', '.join('%s %d건' % kv for kv in tool_atk[tool].most_common()))
        if found:
            lines.append('▶ 존재 확인된 경로(404 아님): ' + ', '.join('%s(%s)' % (u, s) for u, s in list(found.items())[:15]))
        summ = '%s → %s 요청 %d개' % (', '.join(sorted(e['cli'])), ', '.join(sorted(e['srv'])), e['n'])
        if tool in DIRBUST_TOOLS | SCANNER_TOOLS:
            summ += ' (404 %d개), 찾은 경로: %s' % (codes.get(404, 0), ', '.join(list(found)[:5]) or '없음')
        if tool == 'sqlmap':
            summ += ', 파라미터 %s, %s' % (params.most_common(1)[0][0] if params else '?', 'DB 에러 응답 → 취약 가능성 높음' if vuln else 'DB 에러 없음')
        out.append(Finding(prio, title, summ,
                           lines, ev=e['ev'], wf=[('http.user_agent contains "%s"' % ua_filter_text(e['ua']), '%s 요청' % tool),
                                                  ('http.response.code != 404 && http.response', '404가 아닌 응답(찾아낸 경로)')]))

    # UA 없이도 404 비율로 디렉터리 무차별 대입 판단
    for key, rs in by_pair.items():
        if key in tools_by_pair and tools_by_pair[key] & (DIRBUST_TOOLS | SCANNER_TOOLS | {'sqlmap', 'Hydra'}):
            continue
        n404 = sum(1 for c, q, r in rs if r and r['code'] == 404)
        if len(rs) >= 30 and n404 >= len(rs) * 0.5:
            found = [(req_url(c, q), r['code']) for c, q, r in rs if r and r['code'] != 404][:15]
            out.append(Finding(21, '디렉터리/파일 무차별 대입(dirb/gobuster 류)', '%s → %s 요청 %d개 중 404 %d개' % (key[0], key[1], len(rs), n404),
                               ['짧은 시간에 존재하지 않는 경로를 대량 요청 → 숨은 페이지 찾기',
                                '▶ 존재 확인된 경로: ' + (', '.join('%s(%s)' % (u, s) for u, s in found) or '없음')],
                               ev=[c.tl[0][3] for c, q, r in rs[:15] if c.tl], wf=[('http.response.code == 404', '없는 경로 응답')]))

    if atk:
        lines, ev = [], []
        for name, items in atk.items():
            c, q, r = items[0]
            lines.append('▶ %s: %d건  예) %s %s → %s' % (name, len(items), q['method'], cut(_unq(req_url(c, q)), 90),
                                                      ('%d' % r['code']) if r else '응답없음'))
            ev += [q.get('frame') or c.tl[0][3] for c, q, r in items[:5] if c.tl]
        srcs = sorted({c.cli[0] for items in atk.values() for c, q, r in items})
        dsts = sorted({c.srv[0] for items in atk.values() for c, q, r in items})
        out.append(Finding(18, '웹 공격 페이로드 (%s)' % ', '.join(list(atk)[:3]), '%s → %s: %s' % (
            ', '.join(srcs), ', '.join(dsts), ', '.join('%s %d건' % (k, len(v)) for k, v in atk.items())),
            ['공격자 %s → 웹 서버 %s' % (', '.join(srcs), ', '.join(dsts))] + lines, ev=ev,
            wf=[('http.request.uri contains "%27" || http.request.uri contains "script" || http.request.uri contains ".."', '대표적인 공격 요청')]))

    # ── 4) nmap 세부 기능 / hping3 ───────────────────────────────
    modes, mev = collections.OrderedDict(), collections.defaultdict(list)
    for p in main:
        if p.proto == 1 and p.itype == 8 and p.icode == 9:
            modes['OS 탐지(-O): ICMP Echo code=9 프로브'] = p.src; mev['os'].append(p.idx)
        elif p.proto == 6 and p.flags is not None and p.flags & 0x02 and b'\x08\x0a\xff\xff\xff\xff' in p.opts:
            modes['OS 탐지(-O): TCP 타임스탬프 0xFFFFFFFF 프로브'] = p.src; mev['os'].append(p.idx)
        elif p.proto == 6 and p.win == 31337:
            modes['OS 탐지(-O): 윈도우 31337 프로브'] = p.src; mev['os'].append(p.idx)
        elif p.proto == 17 and len(p.data) == 300 and p.data == b'C' * 300:
            modes['OS 탐지(-O): UDP "C"×300 프로브'] = p.src; mev['os'].append(p.idx)
        elif p.proto == 6 and p.data in (b'GET / HTTP/1.0\r\n\r\n', b'OPTIONS / HTTP/1.0\r\n\r\n', b'OPTIONS / RTSP/1.0\r\n\r\n',
                                         b'HELP\r\n', b'\r\n\r\n', b'HEAD / HTTP/1.0\r\n\r\n'):
            modes['서비스 버전 탐지(-sV): nmap 기본 프로브 "%s"' % p.data.strip().decode()[:20]] = p.src; mev['sv'].append(p.idx)
        elif p.proto == 6 and p.data.startswith(b'SSH-') and b'Nmap' in p.data[:40]:
            modes['스크립트 스캔(NSE): SSH 배너 Nmap-SSH'] = p.src; mev['sv'].append(p.idx)
    if 'nmap NSE' in ua_hits:
        modes['스크립트 스캔(NSE): User-Agent "Nmap Scripting Engine"'] = next(iter(ua_hits['nmap NSE']['cli']))
        mev['sv'] += ua_hits['nmap NSE']['ev'][:5]
    if modes:
        kinds = sorted({m.split(':')[0] for m in modes})
        out.append(Finding(31, 'nmap ' + ' + '.join(kinds), '%s 가 nmap %s 수행' % (', '.join(sorted(set(modes.values()))), ', '.join(kinds)),
                           ['▶ ' + m for m in modes] +
                           ['  -O: 응답 패킷의 TTL/윈도우/옵션으로 대상 OS를 맞히는 기능',
                            '  -sV: 열린 포트에 프로브를 보내 서비스 이름·버전 확인 / -sC, -A: NSE 스크립트 실행'],
                           ev=mev['os'][:15] + mev['sv'][:15],
                           wf=[('icmp.code == 9 || tcp.window_size == 31337 || tcp.options.timestamp.tsval == 4294967295', 'OS 탐지 프로브'),
                               ('http.user_agent contains "Nmap"', 'NSE 요청')]))

    hp = [p for p in main if p.proto == 6 and p.flags == 0x02 and p.win == 512 and not p.opts]
    if len(hp) >= 3:
        srcs = collections.Counter(p.src for p in hp)
        dports = {p.dport for p in hp}
        kind = 'SYN Flood' if len(srcs) > 20 or len(dports) <= 2 and len(hp) > 50 else '포트 스캔' if len(dports) >= 5 else 'SYN 전송'
        out.append(Finding(23, 'hping3 사용 흔적 (%s)' % kind, 'SYN 패킷 %d개 (윈도우 512, TCP 옵션 없음 = hping3 기본값)' % len(hp),
                           ['▶ 도구: hping3 — 패킷을 직접 조립해 보내는 도구 (DoS, 방화벽 테스트, 포트 스캔)',
                            '  근거: SYN 윈도우 크기 512 + TCP 옵션 없음 (일반 OS는 64240/65535 + MSS 등 옵션 포함)',
                            '  출발지 %d개 %s, 목적지 포트 %s' % (len(srcs), '(--rand-source 로 IP 위조)' if len(srcs) > 20 else short_list(srcs, 3),
                                                     short_list(sorted(dports), 10))],
                           ev=[p.idx for p in hp[:20]], wf=[('tcp.flags == 0x002 && tcp.window_size == 512', 'hping3 기본 SYN')]))

    # ── 5) 셸 세션(리버스/바인드 셸), Metasploit, 백도어 ───────────
    for c in conns:
        if not (c.bytes[0] or c.bytes[1]) or c.service in ('SSH', 'TLS(HTTPS 등)', 'Telnet', 'FTP', 'HTTP', 'SMTP', 'POP3', 'IMAP'):
            continue                      # 정상 원격 접속 프로토콜은 셸 탐지 대상에서 제외 (Telnet 로그인 등)
        s0, s1 = c.stream(0), c.stream(1)
        m0, m1 = SHELL_RX.search(s0), SHELL_RX.search(s1)
        if not (m0 or m1):
            if c.srv[1] == 4444 or b'metsrv' in s1 or b'stdapi_' in s0 + s1 or b'core_channel' in s0 + s1:
                out.append(Finding(9, 'Metasploit 페이로드/Meterpreter 의심', '%s ↔ %s (%s바이트)' % (ep(c.cli), ep(c.srv), format(c.bytes[0] + c.bytes[1], ',')),
                                   ['포트 4444 = Metasploit 기본 리스너 포트' if 4444 in (c.srv[1], c.cli[1]) else '',
                                    'Meterpreter 문자열(metsrv/stdapi/core_channel) 발견' if (b'metsrv' in s1 or b'stdapi_' in s0 + s1) else ''],
                                   ev=c.idxs[:20], wf=[('tcp.port == %d' % c.srv[1], '이 세션')]))
            continue
        # 셸 출력이 나오는 쪽 = 피해자, 명령을 보내는 쪽 = 공격자
        out_dir = 0 if m0 else 1
        victim = c.cli if out_dir == 0 else c.srv
        attacker = c.srv if out_dir == 0 else c.cli
        reverse = out_dir == 0          # 피해자가 먼저 접속(클라이언트) = 리버스 셸
        cmds = []
        for line in (s1 if out_dir == 0 else s0).decode('utf-8', 'replace').splitlines():
            l = line.strip()
            if l and (CMD_RX.match(l) or len(l) < 60) and len(cmds) < 20:
                cmds.append(l)
        outtxt = (s0 if out_dir == 0 else s1).decode('utf-8', 'replace')
        user = re.search(r'uid=\d+\(([\w.-]+)\)', outtxt)
        lines = ['▶ %s: 피해자 %s 가 공격자 %s 에게 셸을 넘겨줌' % ('리버스 셸' if reverse else '바인드 셸(접속형 셸)', ep(victim), ep(attacker)),
                 '  %s' % ('피해자가 공격자의 리스닝 포트(%d)로 먼저 접속 → 방화벽 우회용 리버스 셸 (nc -lvnp %d 등으로 대기)' % (attacker[1], attacker[1])
                          if reverse else '공격자가 피해자의 열린 포트(%d)에 접속해 셸을 얻음' % victim[1]),
                 '▶ 공격자가 실행한 명령: ' + (', '.join(cmds[:12]) or '확인 안 됨')]
        if user:
            lines.append('▶ 획득 권한: %s%s' % (user.group(1), ' (관리자! 최고 권한)' if user.group(1) == 'root' else ''))
        if 4444 in (c.srv[1], c.cli[1]):
            lines.append('  포트 4444 = Metasploit/netcat 리버스 셸 기본 포트')
        if c.srv[1] == 1524:
            lines.append('  포트 1524 = Metasploitable ingreslock 백도어(루트 셸)')
        out.append(Finding(9, '%s 획득 (원격 명령 실행)' % ('리버스 셸' if reverse else '바인드 셸'),
                           '%s → %s, 명령: %s%s' % (ep(c.cli), ep(c.srv), ', '.join(cmds[:5]), ' / 권한 %s' % user.group(1) if user else ''),
                           lines, ev=[t[3] for t in c.tl[:30]], wf=[('tcp.port == %d' % c.srv[1], '셸 세션 → Follow TCP Stream')]))

    ftp_user_bd = [(c, ix) for c in conns if c.service == 'FTP' for ts, d, line, ix in lines_in_time(c)
                   if d == 0 and re.match(r'(?i)USER \S*:\)', line)]
    if ftp_user_bd:
        c0 = ftp_user_bd[0][0]
        hit6200 = [c for c in conns if c.srv[0] == c0.srv[0] and c.srv[1] == 6200]
        opened = any(c.synack for c in hit6200)
        out.append(Finding(8, 'vsftpd 2.3.4 백도어 공격 (CVE-2011-2523)', 'USER 이름 끝에 ":)" 전송%s' % (
            ' → 6200 포트 %s' % ('열림(백도어 셸 획득)' if opened else '접속 시도(닫힘)') if hit6200 else ''),
            ['▶ FTP USER 명령의 사용자 이름이 ":)" 로 끝나면 vsftpd 2.3.4 백도어가 6200 포트에 루트 셸을 연다',
             '  Metasploit: exploit/unix/ftp/vsftpd_234_backdoor',
             '▶ 결과: ' + ('6200 포트 연결 성공 → 백도어 셸 획득' if opened else '6200 포트 닫힘(RST) → 실패' if hit6200 else '6200 접속 흔적 없음')],
            ev=[ix for c, ix in ftp_user_bd[:3]] + [i for c in hit6200 for i in c.idxs[:4]],
            wf=[('ftp.request.arg contains ":)" || tcp.port == 6200', '백도어 트리거와 6200 포트')]))
    for c in conns:
        s0 = c.stream(0)[:2000]
        if c.srv[1] in (6667, 6697) and s0.startswith(b'AB;'):
            out.append(Finding(8, 'UnrealIRCd 3.2.8.1 백도어 공격', '%s 에 "AB;" 명령 주입' % ep(c.srv),
                               ['▶ 클라이언트가 "AB;" 로 시작하는 데이터 전송 → 백도어로 OS 명령 실행', '  내용: ' + preview(s0, 120)],
                               ev=c.idxs[:10], wf=[('tcp.port == %d' % c.srv[1], '')]))
        if b'/=`nohup' in s0 or b'/=`' in s0 and c.srv[1] in (139, 445):
            out.append(Finding(8, 'Samba usermap_script 공격 (CVE-2007-2447)', '%s 로 셸 메타문자 사용자명 전송' % ep(c.srv),
                               ['▶ 사용자 이름에 "/=`nohup ...`" → Samba가 명령 실행', '  Metasploit: exploit/multi/samba/usermap_script'],
                               ev=c.idxs[:10], wf=[('smb || tcp.port == 139 || tcp.port == 445', '')]))
        if c.srv[1] == 3632 and b'DIST' in s0[:20]:
            out.append(Finding(8, 'distcc 원격 명령 실행 (CVE-2004-2687)', '%s 에 DIST 명령' % ep(c.srv),
                               ['▶ distccd에 컴파일 명령 대신 셸 명령 전달', '  내용: ' + preview(s0, 120)], ev=c.idxs[:10],
                               wf=[('tcp.port == 3632', '')]))

    # ── 6) TLS 접속 대상(SNI) / AI API ───────────────────────────
    snis = collections.OrderedDict()
    for c in conns:
        if c.service == 'TLS(HTTPS 등)' or (c.stream(0)[:1] == b'\x16'):
            n = tls_sni(c)
            if n:
                snis.setdefault(n, []).append(c)
    ai = [(n, name) for n in snis for rx, name in AI_API if re.search(rx, n)]
    if snis:
        lines = ['%s  ← %s (%d회, 첫 프레임 #%d)' % (n, ', '.join(sorted({ep(x.cli).rsplit(':', 1)[0] for x in cs})), len(cs), cs[0].idxs[0])
                 for n, cs in list(snis.items())[:20]]
        if ai:
            lines.insert(0, '▶ AI(LLM) API 접속: ' + ', '.join('%s(%s)' % a for a in ai) +
                         ' → ARTEX 같은 AI 에이전트 공격 도구가 LLM을 호출한 흔적일 수 있음')
        out.append(Finding(45 if ai else 75, 'AI(LLM) API 접속' if ai else 'TLS(HTTPS) 접속 대상',
                           ('AI API: ' + ', '.join(a[1] for a in ai)) if ai else ', '.join(list(snis)[:4]), lines,
                           ev=[x.idxs[3] if len(x.idxs) > 3 else x.idxs[0] for cs in snis.values() for x in cs][:20],
                           wf=[('tls.handshake.extensions_server_name', 'TLS 접속 도메인(SNI)')]))

    # ── 7) ARTEX 종합 판정 ───────────────────────────────────────
    art = []
    for tool in ('ARTEX', 'ARTEX(norma SDK)'):
        if tool in ua_hits:
            e = ua_hits[tool]
            art.append(('▶ User-Agent "%s" %d회 → %s' % (e['ua'], e['n'], e['desc']), e['ev']))
            if tool == 'ARTEX':
                art.append(('  enrich 프로브 대상 %d개 호스트: %s' % (len(e['srv']), ', '.join(sorted(e['srv']))), []))
    panel = [c for c in conns if 8787 in (c.srv[1],) and c.bytes[1]]
    if panel:
        art.append(('▶ 8787 포트 웹 접속 %d건 → ARTEX 관리 화면(기본 포트 8787) 가능성' % len(panel), [c.idxs[0] for c in panel[:5]]))
    if art and ai:
        art.append(('▶ AI API 접속 동반: ' + ', '.join(a[1] for a in ai), []))
    if art:
        others = [t for t in ua_hits if not t.startswith('ARTEX')]
        modes_s = ', '.join(sorted({m.split(':')[0] for m in modes})) if modes else ''
        lines = [a for a, _ in art] + [
            '  ARTEX = LLM 기반 자율 침투 테스트 도구: Planner 에이전트가 계획 → Worker 에이전트가 Kali 도구(nmap, curl 등) 실행',
            '  따라서 같은 출발지에서 nmap 스캔, curl 요청, 웹 공격 페이로드가 짧은 간격으로 이어지는 것이 특징',
            '▶ 같이 관찰된 도구/공격: ' + (', '.join(others + ([modes_s] if modes_s else []) + list(atk)) or '없음')]
        out.append(Finding(7, 'ARTEX (AI 자율 침투 테스트 도구) 사용 흔적', 'ARTEX 지문 발견: ' + ', '.join(
            t for t in ('artex-enrich', 'norma SDK', '8787 관리화면') if
            (t == 'artex-enrich' and 'ARTEX' in ua_hits) or (t == 'norma SDK' and 'ARTEX(norma SDK)' in ua_hits) or (t == '8787 관리화면' and panel)),
            lines, ev=[i for _, ev in art for i in ev][:30],
            wf=[('http.user_agent contains "artex" || http.user_agent contains "norma"', 'ARTEX 요청'), ('tcp.port == 8787', 'ARTEX 관리 화면')]))
    return out


def l2_attack_findings(pkts):
    """DNS 스푸핑, DHCP 고갈, MAC 플러딩 (배경 트래픽까지 포함해서 봐야 하는 것)"""
    out = []
    resp = collections.defaultdict(list)
    for p in pkts:
        if p.proto == 17 and p.sport == 53 and p.data:
```
