            m = parse_dns(p.data)
            if m and m['qr'] and m['q']:
                resp[(m['id'], m['q'][0][0])].append((p, tuple(sorted(v for _, t, v in m['a'] if t in ('A', 'AAAA')))))
    for (tid, name), lst in resp.items():
        ans = {a for _, a in lst if a}
        if len(ans) >= 2:
            lines = ['같은 질의(ID 0x%04x, %s)에 서로 다른 응답 %d개 → 먼저 도착한 가짜 응답으로 속이는 DNS 스푸핑' % (tid, name, len(ans))]
            for p, a in lst:
                lines.append('  #%d %s → %s : %s' % (p.idx, p.src, p.dst, ', '.join(a) or '(답 없음)'))
            out.append(Finding(10, 'DNS 스푸핑 (ettercap dns_spoof 등)', '%s 에 대한 응답이 %s 로 엇갈림' % (name, ' / '.join(', '.join(a) for a in ans)),
                               lines, ev=[p.idx for p, _ in lst], wf=[('dns.id == 0x%04x' % tid, '같은 ID의 질의/응답')]))
    disc = [p for p in pkts if p.proto == 17 and p.dport == 67 and len(p.data) > 240 and p.data[0] == 1]
    macs = {p.data[28:34] for p in disc}
    if len(disc) >= 30 and len(macs) >= 20:
        out.append(Finding(12, 'DHCP 고갈(Starvation) 공격', 'DHCP 요청 %d개, 서로 다른 MAC %d개' % (len(disc), len(macs)),
                           ['▶ 가짜 MAC으로 DHCP 주소를 모두 받아가 정상 사용자가 IP를 못 받게 함 (yersinia, dhcpstarv 등)'],
                           ev=[p.idx for p in disc[:20]], wf=[('dhcp.option.dhcp == 1', 'DHCP Discover')]))
    srcm = {p.eth_src for p in pkts if p.eth_src}
    if len(srcm) >= 500:
        out.append(Finding(12, 'MAC 플러딩 (macof 등)', '서로 다른 출발지 MAC %d개' % len(srcm),
                           ['▶ 스위치의 MAC 테이블을 가득 채워 허브처럼 동작하게 만들어 스니핑하는 공격'],
                           ev=[p.idx for p in pkts[:20]], wf=[('eth.src', '')]))
    return out


# ── 인증 기반 서비스(FTP/Telnet/POP3/IMAP/SMTP) 판정 ─────────────────
def auth_findings(svc, cip, sip, sport, cs, results, verbose):
    attempts = sorted((a for r in results for a in r['attempts']), key=lambda a: a['ts'])
    banner = next((r['banner'] for r in results if r.get('banner')), '')
    conc = max_concurrency(cs)
    users = list(collections.OrderedDict.fromkeys(str(a['user']) for a in attempts))
    pwds = list(collections.OrderedDict.fromkeys(str(a['pass']) for a in attempts if a['pass'] is not None))
    succ = [a for a in attempts if a['result'] == '성공']
    fail = [a for a in attempts if a['result'] == '실패']
    head = ['%s %s → %s:%d  (세션 %d개, %s ~ %s)' % (svc, cip, sip, sport, len(cs),
                                                   fmt_ts(min(c.start for c in cs)), fmt_ts(max(c.end for c in cs), False))]
    if banner:
        head.append('서버 배너: ' + banner)
    brute = len(attempts) >= 5 or (len(cs) >= 5 and len(attempts) >= 3) or len(pwds) >= 4
    if brute:
        reasons = []
        multi_user = any(len({str(a['user']) for a in r['attempts']}) >= 2 for r in results)
        per_conn = len(attempts) / float(max(1, len(cs)))
        if conc >= 4 and multi_user:
            tool = 'Ncrack'
            reasons.append('동시 %d개 세션 + 한 세션 안에서 여러 계정을 바꿔가며 시도(Ncrack 특징)' % conc)
        elif conc >= 4:
            tool = 'Hydra'
            reasons.append('동시에 %d개 세션을 병렬로 열고 세션마다 1회씩 시도(Hydra 기본 16 task 특징)' % conc)
        elif len(cs) >= 5 and conc <= 2:
            tool = 'Medusa'
            reasons.append('동시 접속 %d개 이하로 순차 시도(Medusa 기본 동작, Hydra는 16개 병렬) — Metasploit 모듈도 비슷' % conc)
        else:
            tool = '자동화 도구(Hydra/Medusa 등)'
        reasons.append('짧은 시간에 %d회 로그인 시도(세션당 %.1f회)' % (len(attempts), per_conn))
        dur = max(c.end for c in cs) - min(c.start for c in cs)
        lines = head + [
            '▶ 공격: %s를 이용한 %s 무차별 대입(Brute Force / 사전 대입) 공격' % (tool, svc),
            '  근거: ' + '; '.join(reasons) + ' (공격 시간 약 %.1f초)' % dur,
            '  시도 %d회 = 성공 %d / 실패 %d / 응답없음 %d' % (len(attempts), len(succ), len(fail),
                                                     len(attempts) - len(succ) - len(fail)),
            '  시도한 계정: ' + short_list(users),
            '  시도한 비밀번호: ' + short_list(pwds, 20)]
        if succ:
            for a in succ:
                lines.append('▶ 결과: 공격 성공!  계정 %s / 비밀번호 %s  (%s, %s, 출발포트 %d)' % (
                    a['user'], a['pass'], a['msg'], fmt_ts(a['ts'], False), a['conn'].cli[1]))
            summ = '★성공 %s / %s  (%s %d회 시도)' % (succ[0]['user'], succ[0]['pass'], svc, len(attempts))
        else:
            lines.append('▶ 결과: 공격 실패 (성공 응답 없음)')
            summ = '실패 (%s %d회 시도, 계정 %s)' % (svc, len(attempts), short_list(users, 4))
        lines.append('  [계정별 결과]')
        for u in users:
            mine = [a for a in attempts if str(a['user']) == u]
            ok = [a for a in mine if a['result'] == '성공']
            pw_list = list(collections.OrderedDict.fromkeys(str(a['pass']) for a in mine))
            lines.append('   %-12s %d회 (비밀번호 %s) → %s' % (u, len(mine), short_list(pw_list, 8),
                         ('★성공: %s  #%d' % (ok[0]['pass'], ok[0]['ix'][-1] if ok[0]['ix'] else 0)) if ok else '전부 실패'))
        if verbose:
            lines.append('  [시도 목록 전체]')
            for a in attempts:
                lines.append('   %s  %-12s / %-15s → %s  %s' % (fmt_ts(a['ts'], False), a['user'], a['pass'],
                                                              a['result'] or '응답없음', a['msg'][:40]))
        return [Finding(20, '%s %s 무차별 대입 공격' % (tool, svc), summ, lines,
            ev=[i for a in succ for i in a['conn'].idxs] + [i for a in fail[:3] for i in a['ix']],
            groups=[('성공한 세션 전체 (출발포트 %d) — %s / %s' % (a['conn'].cli[1], a['user'], a['pass']), a['conn'].idxs) for a in succ[:2]]
                   + [('실패한 시도 예시 %d건 (USER → 331 → PASS → 530)' % len(fail[:3]), [i for a in fail[:3] for i in a['ix']])], wf=[(WF_AUTH.get(svc, svc.lower()), '로그인 시도/결과 전체')] + ([('tcp.port == %d' % succ[0]['conn'].cli[1], '성공한 세션만 → 우클릭 Follow > TCP Stream')] if succ else []))]

    # 일반 로그인 세션
    lines = list(head)
    for a in attempts:
        lines.append('▶ 로그인: 계정 %s / 비밀번호 %s → %s  %s' % (a['user'], a['pass'], a['result'] or '응답없음', a['msg']))
    for r in results:
        for item in r.get('cmds', []):
            if isinstance(item, list):
                lines.append('  명령: %-30s → %s' % (item[1], item[2]))
            else:
                lines.append('  명령: ' + item)
        for cmd in r.get('commands', []):
            lines.append('  입력한 명령: ' + cmd)
    if attempts:
        a = succ[0] if succ else attempts[-1]
        summ = '계정 %s / 비밀번호 %s → 로그인 %s' % (a['user'], a['pass'], a['result'] or '응답없음')
    else:
        summ = '%s 접속 (로그인 정보 없음)' % svc
    return [Finding(50, '%s 접속' % svc, summ, lines,
            ev=[i for c in cs for i in c.idxs], wf=[(WF_PROTO.get(svc, svc.lower()) if sport == STD_PORT.get(svc) else 'tcp.port == %d' % sport, '%s 패킷' % svc),
                                                   ('tcp.port == %d' % cs[0].cli[1], '이 세션만 → Follow TCP Stream')])]


def http_findings(cip, sip, sport, cs, verbose):
    pairs = []
    for c in cs:
        pairs += [(c, q, r) for q, r in analyze_http(c)]
    if not pairs:
        return []
    uas = collections.Counter(q['hdr'].get('user-agent', '') for _, q, _ in pairs)
    hydra_ua = any('hydra' in ua.lower() for ua in uas)
    tool_ua = [ua for ua in uas if re.search(r'(?i)hydra|nikto|sqlmap|dirb|gobuster|wfuzz|nmap|curl|wget|python|burp|zgrab|masscan|ffuf', ua)]
    creds = [(c, q, r, cr) for c, q, r in pairs for cr in http_creds(q)]
    lines = ['HTTP %s → %s:%d  (세션 %d개, 요청 %d개)' % (cip, sip, sport, len(cs), len(pairs))]
    for ua, n in uas.most_common(3):
        lines.append('User-Agent: %s%s' % (ua or '(없음)', ' (×%d)' % n if n > 1 else ''))
    servers = {r['hdr'].get('server') for _, _, r in pairs if r and r['hdr'].get('server')}
    if servers:
        lines.append('웹 서버: ' + ', '.join(servers))
    paths = collections.Counter(q['uri'].split('?')[0] for _, q, _, _ in creds)
    same_path = bool(paths) and paths.most_common(1)[0][1] >= len(creds) * 0.8
    brute = hydra_ua or (len(creds) >= 5 and same_path) or sum(1 for _, _, r in pairs if r and r['code'] == 401) >= 5
    if brute:
        def rsig(r):
            return (r['code'], r['raw_len'], http_title(r['body']), r['hdr'].get('location', '')) if r else None
        sig = collections.Counter(rsig(r) for _, _, r, _ in creds)
        common = sig.most_common(1)[0][0] if sig else None
        outliers = [(c, q, r, cr) for c, q, r, cr in creds if r and rsig(r) != common]
        if not creds:   # Basic 인증 없이 401만 반복되는 경우
            outliers = [(c, q, r, ('?', '?', '?')) for c, q, r in pairs if r and r['code'] not in (401, 403)]
        lines.append('▶ 공격: %s HTTP 로그인 무차별 대입 공격 (시도 %d회)' % ('Hydra를 이용한' if hydra_ua else '자동화 도구로', len(creds) or len(pairs)))
        if hydra_ua:
            lines.append('  근거: User-Agent에 "Hydra" 문자열')
        lines.append('  시도한 계정: ' + short_list(collections.OrderedDict.fromkeys(str(cr[1]) for *_, cr in creds)))
        lines.append('  시도한 비밀번호: ' + short_list(collections.OrderedDict.fromkeys(str(cr[2]) for *_, cr in creds), 20))
        okset = {id(q) for c, q, r, cr in outliers} if outliers and len(outliers) < len(creds or pairs) else set()
        for c, q, r, cr in creds:
            c.__dict__.setdefault('_brute_res', {})[id(q)] = (
                '성공 추정(혼자 다른 응답 %d)' % r['code'] if id(q) in okset else
                ('실패(다른 시도와 같은 응답 %d)' % r['code'] if r else '응답없음'))
        if outliers and len(outliers) < len(creds or pairs):
            for c, q, r, cr in outliers[:5]:
                lines.append('▶ 결과(추정): 혼자 다른 응답을 받은 시도 → 계정 %s / 비밀번호 %s  응답 %d %s, 본문 %dB%s, 프레임 #%d (성공 가능성 높음)' % (
                    cr[1], cr[2], r['code'], r['reason'], r['raw_len'], (', 제목 "%s"' % http_title(r['body'])) if http_title(r['body']) else '', r['frame']))
            summ = '★성공 추정 %s / %s  (HTTP %d회 시도)' % (outliers[0][3][1], outliers[0][3][2], len(creds) or len(pairs))
        else:
            lines.append('▶ 결과(추정): 모든 응답이 동일 → 실패로 보임')
            summ = '실패 추정 (HTTP %d회 시도)' % (len(creds) or len(pairs))
        if verbose:
            for c, q, r, cr in creds:
                lines.append('   %s / %s → %s' % (cr[1], cr[2], '%d %dB' % (r['code'], r['raw_len']) if r else '응답없음'))
        return [Finding(20, ('Hydra ' if hydra_ua else '') + 'HTTP 무차별 대입 공격', summ, lines,
            ev=[i for x in (outliers[:3] or creds[:3]) for i in x[0].idxs], wf=[('http.request.method == "POST" || http.response', 'POST 요청과 응답'), ('http.user_agent contains "Hydra"', 'Hydra가 보낸 요청')])]

    for c, q, r in pairs[: (len(pairs) if verbose else 15)]:
        host = q['hdr'].get('host', sip)
        resp = '%d %s' % (r['code'], r['reason']) if r else '응답 없음'
        lines.append('▶ %s %s → %s  #%d' % (q['method'], req_url(c, q), resp, q['frame']))
        for kind, u, pw in http_creds(q):
            lines.append('    로그인 정보(%s): 계정 %s / 비밀번호 %s → %s' % (kind, u, pw, http_login_result(r, q)))
        if q['method'] in ('POST', 'PUT') and q['body']:
            lines.append('    요청 본문: ' + preview(q['body'], 150))
        if q['hdr'].get('cookie'):
            lines.append('    Cookie: ' + q['hdr']['cookie'][:100])
        if r:
            t = http_title(r['body'])
            ct = r['hdr'].get('content-type', '')
            extra = []
            if t:
                extra.append('제목 "%s"' % t)
            if ct:
                extra.append(ct.split(';')[0])
            extra.append('본문 %dB%s' % (len(r['body']), '(압축해제)' if len(r['body']) != r['raw_len'] else ''))
            if r['hdr'].get('location'):
                extra.append('이동 → ' + r['hdr']['location'])
            lines.append('    응답: ' + ', '.join(extra))
    if len(pairs) > 15 and not verbose:
        lines.append('  … 요청 %d개 더 ([v] 자세히 켜면 전체)' % (len(pairs) - 15))
    if tool_ua:
        lines.append('※ 도구 User-Agent 감지: ' + short_list(sorted({re.sub(r'\(Test:[^)]*\)', '', u).strip() for u in tool_ua}), 3))
    q0, r0 = pairs[0][1], pairs[0][2]
    summ = '%s %s → %s' % (q0['method'], req_url(pairs[0][0], q0),
                                    ('%d %s' % (r0['code'], r0['reason'])) if r0 else '응답없음')
    if servers:
        summ += ' (%s)' % ', '.join(servers)
    if len(pairs) > 1:
        summ += ' 외 %d건' % (len(pairs) - 1)
    if creds:
        best = next((x for x in creds if '성공' in http_login_result(x[2], x[1])), creds[-1])
        summ += ' / 로그인: %s / %s → %s' % (best[3][1], best[3][2], http_login_result(best[2], best[1]))
    return [Finding(55, 'HTTP 웹 접속', summ, lines,
            ev=[t[3] for c in cs for t in c.tl], wf=[('http', 'HTTP 요청/응답'), ('http.request', '요청만')])]


def ssh_findings(cip, sip, sport, cs, verbose):
    def banner(c, d):
        s = c.stream(d)
        return s.split(b'\n', 1)[0].strip().decode('latin-1') if s.startswith(b'SSH-') else ''
    cb = next((banner(c, 0) for c in cs if banner(c, 0)), '')
    sb = next((banner(c, 1) for c in cs if banner(c, 1)), '')
    lines = ['SSH %s → %s:%d  (세션 %d개, %s ~ %s)' % (cip, sip, sport, len(cs), fmt_ts(min(c.start for c in cs)),
                                                    fmt_ts(max(c.end for c in cs), False)),
             '클라이언트: %s' % (cb or '?'), '서버: %s' % (sb or '?')]
    conc = max_concurrency(cs)
    rawb = next((c.stream(0).split(b'\n', 1)[0] for c in cs if c.stream(0).startswith(b'SSH-')), b'')
    tool = next(((t, why) for rx, t, why in SSH_CLIENTS if re.search(rx, cb)), None)
    if not tool and cb.startswith('SSH-2.0-OpenSSH_7.1') and not rawb.endswith(b'\r') and len(cs) >= 3:
        tool = ('Ncrack', '배너가 고정값 SSH-2.0-OpenSSH_7.1 이고 줄끝에 \\r 이 없음(Ncrack 특징)')
    auth = {id(c): ssh_auth(c) for c in cs}
    tried = [c for c in cs if auth[id(c)] and auth[id(c)]['stage'] == '인증']
    succ = [c for c in tried if auth[id(c)]['success']]
    n_fail = sum(auth[id(c)]['fails'] for c in tried)
    attacky = tool and tool[0] not in ('PuTTY',)
    if len(cs) >= 5 or attacky or n_fail >= 4:
        tname = tool[0] if tool else ('Hydra' if conc >= 4 else '자동화 도구')
        lines.append('▶ 공격: %s를 이용한 SSH 무차별 대입 공격 (세션 %d개, 동시 최대 %d개)' % (tname, len(cs), conc))
        if tool:
            lines.append('  근거: ' + tool[1])
        lines.append('  인증까지 간 세션 %d개, 인증 실패 응답 약 %d회' % (len(tried), n_fail))
        lines.append('  SSH는 암호화되어 시도한 계정/비밀번호 자체는 패킷으로 확인 불가')
        if succ:
            for c in succ[:3]:
                a = auth[id(c)]
                lines.append('▶ 결과: 로그인 성공! 출발포트 %d 세션 (프레임 #%d)' % (c.cli[1], a['succ_frame']))
                lines.append('  근거: 인증 실패 응답은 %s바이트인데 이 세션에서만 %d바이트짜리 작은 응답'
                             '(= SSH_MSG_USERAUTH_SUCCESS, SERVICE_ACCEPT %d바이트보다 작음)' % (a['fail_size'] or '?', a['succ_size'], a['accept']))
            summ = '★성공 (SSH %d세션, 출발포트 %d, #%d) — 비번은 암호화' % (len(cs), succ[0].cli[1], auth[id(succ[0])]['succ_frame'])
        else:
            lines.append('▶ 결과: 실패로 보임 — 모든 인증 응답이 실패 크기')
            summ = '실패 (SSH %d세션)' % len(cs)
        return [Finding(20, '%s SSH 무차별 대입 공격' % tname, summ, lines,
            ev=[i for c in (succ[:1] or cs[:1]) for i in c.idxs],
            groups=[('성공 세션 (출발포트 %d)' % c.cli[1], c.idxs) for c in succ[:2]] or [(None, cs[0].idxs)],
            wf=[('tcp.dstport == %d && tcp.flags.syn == 1 && tcp.flags.ack == 0' % sport, 'SYN 개수 = 접속 세션 수'),
                ('ssh.protocol', '클라이언트/서버 버전 배너')] + ([('tcp.port == %d' % succ[0].cli[1], '성공 세션 → 마지막 작은 패킷 확인')] if succ else []))]
    res_s = ''
    for c in cs:
        a = auth[id(c)]
        lines.append('세션 %s:%d  %.1f초, 클라→서버 %dB/%d패킷, 서버→클라 %dB/%d패킷%s' % (
            c.cli[0], c.cli[1], c.end - c.start, c.bytes[0], c.pkts[0], c.bytes[1], c.pkts[1],
            ', 정상종료(FIN)' if all(c.fin) else ''))
        if a and a['stage'] == '인증':
            if a['success']:
                res_s = '로그인 성공'
                lines.append('▶ 로그인 성공 (프레임 #%d: SERVICE_ACCEPT %d바이트보다 작은 %d바이트 응답 = USERAUTH_SUCCESS)%s' % (
                    a['succ_frame'], a['accept'], a['succ_size'], ', 실패 %d회 후' % a['fails'] if a['fails'] else ''))
            else:
                res_s = res_s or '로그인 실패 추정'
                lines.append('▶ 로그인 실패로 보임 (인증 실패 응답 %d회, 성공 크기 응답 없음)' % a['fails'])
        elif a:
            lines.append('  %s' % a['stage'])
    lines.append('※ SSH는 암호화되어 계정/비밀번호/명령은 패킷에서 볼 수 없음 (성공 여부는 패킷 크기로 추정)')
    return [Finding(56, 'SSH 접속', 'SSH %s → %s (클라 %s / 서버 %s)%s' % (cip, sip, cb.replace('SSH-2.0-', ''),
                                                                     sb.replace('SSH-2.0-', ''), ' → ' + res_s if res_s else ''), lines,
            ev=[i for c in cs for i in c.idxs[:14]], wf=[('ssh', 'SSH 패킷'), ('ssh.protocol', '버전 배너')])]


# ── 스캔 / 스윕 ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
def nmap_discovery_signature(pk):
    """nmap 호스트 탐색 기본 프로브 4종을 보낸 출발지 집합"""
    kinds = collections.defaultdict(set)
    for p in pk:
        if p.proto == 1 and p.itype == 8 and len(p.data) == 0:
            kinds[p.src].add('echo')
        elif p.proto == 1 and p.itype == 13:
            kinds[p.src].add('ts')
        elif p.proto == 6 and p.dport == 443 and p.flags == 0x02:
            kinds[p.src].add('syn443')
        elif p.proto == 6 and p.dport == 80 and p.flags == 0x10 and not p.data:
            kinds[p.src].add('ack80')
    return {s for s, k in kinds.items() if len(k) >= 3}


def sweep_sources(pk):
    tg = collections.defaultdict(set)
    for p in pk:
        if p.proto == 1 and p.itype in (8, 13):
            tg[p.src].add(p.dst)
        elif p.arp and p.arp[0] == 1:
            tg[p.arp[2]].add(p.arp[4])
    return {s for s, t in tg.items() if len(t) >= 10}


def sweep_findings(pk, nmap_srcs):
    out = []
    targets = collections.defaultdict(set)
    methods = collections.defaultdict(set)
    for p in pk:
        if p.proto == 1 and p.itype == 8:
            targets[p.src].add(p.dst); methods[p.src].add('ICMP Echo')
        elif p.proto == 1 and p.itype == 13:
            targets[p.src].add(p.dst); methods[p.src].add('ICMP Timestamp')
        elif p.proto == 6 and p.flags == 0x02 and p.dport == 443:
            targets[p.src].add(p.dst); methods[p.src].add('TCP SYN→443')
        elif p.proto == 6 and p.flags == 0x10 and p.dport == 80 and not p.data:
            targets[p.src].add(p.dst); methods[p.src].add('TCP ACK→80')
        elif p.arp and p.arp[0] == 1:
            targets[p.arp[2]].add(p.arp[4]); methods[p.arp[2]].add('ARP 요청')
    for s, tg in targets.items():
        if len(tg) < 10:
            if not (s in nmap_srcs and len(methods[s]) >= 3):
                continue
            scan_ports = {p.dport for p in pk if p.proto == 6 and p.src == s and p.flags == 0x02}
            if len(scan_ports - {80, 443}) >= 2:
                continue                       # 포트 스캔 앞에 nmap이 하는 호스트 확인 → 포트 스캔 판정에 포함
        alive, others, rep_ev = set(), set(), []
        for p in pk:
            if p.dst == s and p.src and p.src != s:
                if p.proto == 1 and p.itype in (0, 14) or p.proto == 6:
                    (alive if p.src in tg else others).add(p.src)
                    if p.src in tg:
                        rep_ev.append(p.idx)
            if p.arp and p.arp[0] == 2 and p.arp[4] == s:
                alive.add(p.arp[2])
        nm = s in nmap_srcs or 'ARP 요청' in methods[s] and len(tg) > 20
        sk = lambda x: [int(y) if y.isdigit() else 0 for y in x.split('.')]
        lines = ['탐색자 %s → %s' % (s, describe_targets(tg)),
                 '사용한 프로브: ' + ', '.join(sorted(methods[s]))]
        if s in nmap_srcs:
            lines.append('nmap 특징: ICMP Echo(데이터 0바이트) + ICMP Timestamp + TCP 443 SYN + TCP 80 ACK = nmap -sn 기본 프로브')
        lines.append('▶ 살아있는 호스트(응답): %s' % (', '.join(sorted(alive, key=sk)) or '없음'))
        if others:
            lines.append('  (대상 목록 밖에서 응답: %s)' % ', '.join(sorted(others, key=sk)))
        out.append(Finding(35, ('nmap ' if nm else '') + '호스트 탐색(핑 스윕, -sn)',
                           '%s → %s 탐색, 응답 호스트: %s' % (s, describe_targets(tg), ', '.join(sorted(alive, key=sk)) or '없음'),
                           lines,
            ev=[p.idx for p in pk if p.src == s][:8] + rep_ev, wf=[('ip.dst == %s && (icmp.type == 0 || icmp.type == 14 || tcp)' % s, '살아있는 호스트의 응답'), ('ip.src == %s && icmp.type == 8' % s, '핑 스윕 Echo Request')]))
    return out


def stealth_scan_findings(pk, conns):
    live = set()
    for c in conns:
        if c.synack or c.handshake or c.bytes[0] or c.bytes[1]:
            live.add((c.cli, c.srv))
            live.add((c.srv, c.cli))
    names = {0x01: 'FIN 스캔(-sF)', 0x00: 'NULL 스캔(-sN)', 0x29: 'Xmas 스캔(-sX)', 0x10: 'ACK 스캔(-sA)',
             0x11: 'Maimon 스캔(-sM)'}
    by = collections.defaultdict(set)
    byi = collections.defaultdict(list)
    for p in pk:
        if p.proto == 6 and p.flags in names and not p.data and not (p.flags == 0x10 and p.dport == 80) \
                and ((p.src, p.sport), (p.dst, p.dport)) not in live:
            by[(p.src, p.dst, p.flags)].add(p.dport)
            byi[(p.src, p.dst, p.flags)].append(p.idx)
    out = []
    for (a, v, fl), ports in by.items():
        if len(ports) < 6 or (fl == 0x10 and len(ports) < 20):
            continue
        rst = {p.sport for p in pk if p.proto == 6 and p.src == v and p.dst == a and (p.flags or 0) & 0x04}
        if fl == 0x10:
            res = '응답 RST=필터 안 됨(unfiltered): %d개, 무응답=필터됨: %d개' % (len(ports & rst), len(ports - rst))
        else:
            res = '▶ 열림|필터(무응답): %s / 닫힘(RST): %d개' % (ports_str(ports - rst) or '없음', len(ports & rst))
        out.append(Finding(30, 'nmap ' + names[fl], '%s → %s 포트 %d개, %s' % (a, v, len(ports), res),
                           ['공격자 %s → 대상 %s, TCP 플래그 %s 패킷으로 %d개 포트 검사' % (a, v, fl_str(fl), len(ports)), res],
            ev=byi[(a, v, fl)][:30] + [p.idx for p in pk if p.proto == 6 and p.src == v and p.dst == a][:30], wf=[('ip.src == %s && tcp.flags == 0x%03x' % (a, fl), '스캔 패킷'), ('ip.src == %s && tcp.flags.reset == 1' % v, 'RST 응답 = 닫힌 포트')]))
    return out


def ua_filter_text(ua):
    """User-Agent 에서 필터에 넣기 좋은 부분 (괄호·따옴표 없는 첫 덩어리)"""
    parts = [x.strip() for x in re.split(r'[()"\\]', ua) if len(x.strip()) >= 6]
    return (parts[0] if parts else ua.replace('"', ''))[:40]


def banner_of(c):
    """서버가 보낸 첫 응답에서 제품/버전이 보이는 부분"""
    t = c.stream(1)[:400].decode('latin-1', 'replace')
    m = re.search(r'(?im)^server:\s*([^\r\n]+)', t)
    if m:
        return m.group(1).strip()[:50]
    first = t.split('\n', 1)[0].strip()
    return first[:50]


def fl_str(f):
    return ''.join(c for bit, c in [(1, 'F'), (2, 'S'), (4, 'R'), (8, 'P'), (16, 'A'), (32, 'U')] if f & bit) or 'NULL(없음)'


def is_trace_port(port):
    return 33434 <= port <= 33600


def udp_scan_findings(pk):
    by = collections.defaultdict(set)
    for p in pk:
        if p.proto == 17 and p.dport is not None and not is_trace_port(p.dport) and p.sport != 53 and p.dport < 49152:
            by[(p.src, p.dst)].add(p.dport)
    out = []
    unr_n = collections.Counter()
    unr_ports = collections.defaultdict(set)
    udp_resp = collections.defaultdict(set)
    for p in pk:
        if p.proto == 1 and p.itype == 3 and p.icode == 3 and p.inner:
            unr_n[(p.dst, p.src)] += 1
            unr_ports[p.dst].add(p.inner['dport'])
        elif p.proto == 17 and p.sport is not None:
            udp_resp[(p.src, p.dst)].add(p.sport)
    for (a, v), ports in by.items():
        if len(ports) < 4:
            continue
        n_unr = unr_n[(a, v)]
        if len(ports) < 6 and n_unr == 0:
            continue
        unreach = unr_ports[a]
        resp = udp_resp[(v, a)]
        out.append(Finding(31, 'UDP 포트 스캔(nmap -sU)', '%s → %s UDP %d개 포트, 열림: %s' % (a, v, len(ports), ports_str(resp) or '없음'),
                           ['공격자 %s → 대상 %s UDP 포트 %d개' % (a, v, len(ports)),
                            '▶ 열린 포트(UDP 응답): ' + (ports_str(resp) or '없음'),
                            '  닫힌 포트(ICMP Port Unreachable): %d개' % len(unreach & ports),
                            '  열림|필터(무응답): %d개' % len(ports - unreach - resp)],
            ev=[p.idx for p in pk if (p.src == a and p.dst == v and p.proto == 17) or (p.src == v and p.dst == a)][:80], wf=[('ip.addr == %s && (udp || icmp.type == 3)' % v, 'UDP 프로브와 응답')]))
    return out


def traceroute_findings(pk):
    out = []
    udp = collections.defaultdict(list)
    icmp = collections.defaultdict(list)
    for p in pk:
        if p.proto == 17 and p.dport and is_trace_port(p.dport):
            udp[(p.src, p.dst)].append(p)
        elif p.proto == 1 and p.itype == 8:
            icmp[(p.src, p.dst)].append(p)
    cands = []
    for k, ps in udp.items():
        if len(ps) >= 3:
            cands.append((k, ps, 'UDP', 'traceroute (Linux 기본, UDP 33434~ 포트로 TTL을 늘려가며 전송)'))
    for k, ps in icmp.items():
        ttls = {p.ttl for p in ps}
        if len(ttls) >= 3 and min(ttls) <= 5:
            cands.append((k, ps, 'ICMP', 'tracert (Windows) / traceroute -I (ICMP Echo로 TTL을 늘려가며 전송)'))
    for (s, d), ps, mode, desc in cands:
        probe_ttl = {}
        for p in ps:
            probe_ttl[p.dport if mode == 'UDP' else p.icmp_seq] = p.ttl
        # 캡처 지점 앞에 있는 라우터 수(k): Linux traceroute는 TTL마다 포트 3개씩(33434~) → 원래 TTL = (포트-33434)//3+1
        k = 0
        if mode == 'UDP':
            ks = collections.Counter((p.dport - 33434) // 3 + 1 - p.ttl for p in ps)
            kk, n = ks.most_common(1)[0]
            if kk > 0 and n >= 0.8 * len(ps):
                k = kk
        hops, reached, reached_hop = {}, None, None
        for p in pk:
            if p.proto != 1 or p.dst != s or not p.inner or p.inner['dst'] != d:
                if mode == 'ICMP' and p.proto == 1 and p.itype == 0 and p.src == d and p.dst == s:
                    reached = reached or d
                    t = probe_ttl.get(p.icmp_seq)
                    if t is not None:
                        reached_hop = min(reached_hop or 999, t + k)
                continue
            key = p.inner['dport'] if mode == 'UDP' else p.inner['icmp_seq']
            t = probe_ttl.get(key, p.inner['ttl'])
            if p.itype == 11:
                hops.setdefault(t + k, p.src)
            elif p.itype == 3:
                reached = p.src
                # 목적지에 도착했을 때 남은 TTL(안쪽 헤더) → 목적지까지 실제 홉 수 = 원래 TTL - 남은 TTL + 1
                h = t + k - max(1, p.inner['ttl']) + 1
                reached_hop = min(reached_hop or 999, max(1, h))
        last = max([h for h in hops] + [reached_hop or 0, k])
        seq = []
        for n in range(1, last + 1):
            if n <= k:
                seq.append((n, None, '캡처 지점 이전(안 보임)'))
            elif n in hops:
                seq.append((n, hops[n], ''))
            elif reached_hop == n:
                seq.append((n, reached, '도착'))
            else:
                seq.append((n, '*', '응답 없음'))
        if reached_hop and reached_hop in hops and hops[reached_hop] != reached:
            pass
        elif reached and not reached_hop:
            seq.append((last + 1, reached, '도착'))
        for i, (n, ip, note) in enumerate(seq):
            if ip == reached and n == reached_hop:
                seq[i] = (n, ip, '도착')
        visible = [ip for n, ip, note in seq if ip and ip != '*']
        lines = ['%s → 목적지 %s, %s' % (s, d, desc),
                 '프로브 %d개, 캡처 지점에서 본 TTL 값: %s' % (len(ps), sorted({p.ttl for p in ps}))]
        if k:
            lines.append('※ 프로브 포트로 계산한 원래 TTL이 캡처된 TTL보다 %d 큼 → 캡처 지점 앞에 라우터 %d개가 있어 그 홉은 패킷에 안 보임' % (k, k))
        for n, ip, note in seq:
            lines.append('  %d번째 홉: %s%s' % (n, ip or '(보이지 않음)', ('  (%s)' % note) if note else '  (ICMP Time Exceeded 응답)'))
        lines.append('▶ 목적지 도달: %s' % (('예 — %s 가 응답(%s)' % (reached, 'Port Unreachable' if mode == 'UDP' else 'Echo Reply'))
                                          if reached else '확인 안 됨'))
        lines.insert(1, '▶ 홉 %d개: %s' % (len(seq), '  '.join('%d) %s%s' % (n, ip or '(캡처 전)', '(도착)' if note == '도착' else '')
                                                              for n, ip, note in seq)))
        f = Finding(40, 'Traceroute (경로 추적)', '홉 %d개: %s | %s → %s%s' % (
            len(seq), ' → '.join(visible) or '(없음)', s, d, (' (앞 %d홉은 캡처 전이라 안 보임)' % k) if k else ''), lines,
            ev=[p.idx for p in ps] + [p.idx for p in pk if p.proto == 1 and p.dst == s and ((p.inner and p.inner['dst'] == d) or (p.itype == 0 and p.src == d))], wf=[(('icmp.type == 11 || icmp.type == 3 || (ip.src == %s && udp.dstport >= 33434 && udp.dstport <= 33534)' % s) if mode == 'UDP' else ('icmp && ip.addr == %s' % s), '프로브와 Time Exceeded/도착 응답')])
        f.src, f.dst = s, d
        out.append(f)
    return out


def ping_findings(pk, trace_pairs, sweepers):
    reqs = collections.defaultdict(list)
    reps = collections.defaultdict(list)
    for p in pk:
        if p.proto == 1 and p.itype == 8 and not p.frag_off:
            reqs[(p.src, p.dst)].append(p)
        elif p.proto == 1 and p.itype == 0:
            reps[(p.dst, p.src)].append(p)
    out = []
    per_dst = collections.Counter()
    for (s, d), rq in reqs.items():
        per_dst[d] += len(rq)
    unr_by = collections.defaultdict(list)
    for p in pk:
        if p.proto == 1 and p.itype == 3:
            unr_by[p.dst].append(p)
    for (s, d), rq in reqs.items():
        if (s, d) in trace_pairs or s in sweepers or any(p.icode for p in rq) or d.endswith('.255') or d == '255.255.255.255':
            continue
        if per_dst[d] >= 200 and len(rq) < 50:
            continue                                   # 출발지를 위조한 ICMP Flood의 조각 → DoS 쪽에서 처리
        if len(rq) <= 2 and all(len(p.data) == 0 for p in rq):
            continue                                   # nmap 호스트 확인용 단발 프로브
        rp = reps.get((s, d), [])
        if len(rq) >= 200:
            continue                                   # 플러딩은 DoS 쪽에서 처리
        size = len(rq[0].data) + 8
        if rq[0].mf:                                   # 큰 ping → IP 조각으로 나뉨: 조각을 합친 크기
            size = sum(len(x.data) for x in pk if x.src == s and x.dst == d and x.ipid == rq[0].ipid and x.proto == 1 and
                       (x.mf or x.frag_off)) + 8
            if size + 20 > 65535:
                continue                               # Ping of Death → DoS 쪽에서 처리
        payload = rq[0].data
        if b'abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvw' in payload:
            tool = 'Windows ping (데이터 abcdefg… 패턴)'
        elif bytes(range(0x10, 0x30)) in payload:
            tool = 'Linux/Unix ping (데이터 0x10~0x37 패턴)'
        elif not payload:
            tool = '데이터 없는 Echo (nmap/hping 등 도구 가능성)'
        else:
            tool = '알 수 없음'
        loss = 100.0 * (len(rq) - len(rp)) / len(rq)
        lines = ['%s → %s  ICMP Echo Request %d회 / Echo Reply %d회 (손실 %.0f%%)' % (s, d, len(rq), len(rp), max(loss, 0)),
                 'ICMP 크기 %d바이트 (데이터 %d바이트%s), 보낸 도구: %s' % (size, size - 8, ', IP 조각으로 나뉘어 전송 → ping -s 큰 값' if rq[0].mf else '', tool),
                 '기간 %s ~ %s' % (fmt_ts(rq[0].ts), fmt_ts(rq[-1].ts, False))]
        if rp:
            lines.append('응답 TTL %d → 대상 OS 추정: %s' % (rp[0].ttl, guess_os_by_ttl(rp[0].ttl)))
            lines.append('▶ 결과: 대상 %s 살아있음(통신 가능)' % d)
        else:
            unr = unr_by.get(s, [])
            lines.append('▶ 결과: 응답 없음' + (' (ICMP Unreachable from %s)' % unr[0].src if unr else ''))
        out.append(Finding(60, 'Ping (ICMP Echo)', '응답 %d/%d %s | %s → %s' % (len(rp), len(rq), '(살아있음)' if rp else '(응답 없음)', s, d), lines,
            ev=[p.idx for p in rq] + [p.idx for p in rp], wf=[('icmp && ip.addr == %s' % d, 'Echo Request/Reply')]))
    return out


def dos_findings(pk, conns, sweepers=()):
    out = []
    # Land 공격
    land = [p for p in pk if p.proto == 6 and p.src == p.dst and p.sport == p.dport and p.flags and p.flags & 0x02]
    if land:
        out.append(Finding(10, 'Land 공격', '출발지=목적지(%s:%d) SYN %d개' % (land[0].src, land[0].sport, len(land)),
                           ['출발지 IP/포트와 목적지 IP/포트가 같은 SYN 패킷 %d개 → Land Attack' % len(land)],
            ev=[p.idx for p in land], wf=[('ip.src == ip.dst', '출발지 = 목적지')]))
    # SYN Flood
    syn = collections.defaultdict(list)
    for c in conns:
        if c.syn:
            syn[c.srv].append(c)
    for srv, cs in syn.items():
        if len(cs) < 100:
            continue
        done = sum(1 for c in cs if c.handshake)
        srcs = {c.cli[0] for c in cs}
        dur = max(c.start for c in cs) - min(c.start for c in cs) or 1
        if done / len(cs) < 0.3:
            out.append(Finding(10, 'SYN Flooding (DoS)', '%s 대상 SYN %d개, 출발지 %d개%s' % (
                ep(srv), len(cs), len(srcs), '(IP 위조)' if len(srcs) > 20 else ''),
                ['대상 %s 에 SYN %d개 (%.0f개/초), 핸드셰이크 완료 %d개' % (ep(srv), len(cs), len(cs) / dur, done),
                 '출발지 IP %d개: %s' % (len(srcs), short_list(sorted(srcs), 8)),
                 '출발지가 많고 무작위면 IP 스푸핑(위조) 사용'],
            ev=[c.idxs[0] for c in cs[:30]], wf=[('tcp.flags.syn == 1 && tcp.flags.ack == 0 && ip.dst == %s' % srv[0], 'SYN 패킷')]))
    # ICMP / UDP flood, Smurf
    ic = collections.defaultdict(list)
    ud = collections.defaultdict(list)
    for p in pk:
        if p.proto == 1 and p.itype == 8:
            ic[p.dst].append(p)
        elif p.proto == 17 and p.dport != 53:
            ud[p.dst].append(p)
    for dst, ps in ic.items():
        if (dst.endswith('.255') or dst == '255.255.255.255') and ps[0].src not in sweepers and len(ps) >= 3:
            out.append(Finding(10, 'Smurf 공격(의심)', '브로드캐스트 %s 로 Echo Request %d개 (위조 출발지 %s)' % (dst, len(ps), ps[0].src),
                               ['브로드캐스트 주소로 ICMP Echo Request → 응답이 출발지(피해자 %s)로 몰림' % ps[0].src],
            ev=[p.idx for p in ps[:30]], wf=[('icmp.type == 8 && ip.dst == %s' % dst, '브로드캐스트로 보낸 Echo')]))
        elif len(ps) >= 200:
            dur = (ps[-1].ts - ps[0].ts) or 1
            if len(ps) / dur > 50:
                srcs = {p.src for p in ps}
                out.append(Finding(12, 'ICMP Flooding (DoS)', '%s 대상 Echo Request %d개 (%.0f개/초)' % (dst, len(ps), len(ps) / dur),
                                   ['출발지 %d개: %s' % (len(srcs), short_list(sorted(srcs), 6)),
                                    '평균 크기 %d바이트' % (sum(p.iplen for p in ps) // len(ps))],
            ev=[p.idx for p in ps[:30]], wf=[('icmp.type == 8 && ip.dst == %s' % dst, 'Echo Request')]))
    for dst, ps in ud.items():
        if len(ps) >= 300:
            dur = (ps[-1].ts - ps[0].ts) or 1
            if len(ps) / dur > 50:
                out.append(Finding(12, 'UDP Flooding (DoS)', '%s 대상 UDP %d개 (%.0f개/초)' % (dst, len(ps), len(ps) / dur),
                                   ['목적지 포트: ' + short_list(collections.Counter(p.dport for p in ps).most_common(5))],
            ev=[p.idx for p in ps[:30]], wf=[('udp && ip.dst == %s' % dst, 'UDP 패킷')]))
    # IP 조각 공격 (Ping of Death / Teardrop)
    frags = collections.defaultdict(list)
    for p in pk:
        if p.l2 == 'ipv4' and (p.mf or p.frag_off):
            ihl_len = len(p.data) + (8 if p.frag_off == 0 and p.proto in (1, 17) else 0) + (20 if p.frag_off == 0 and p.proto == 6 else 0)
            frags[(p.src, p.dst, p.ipid, p.proto)].append((p.frag_off, ihl_len))
    pod, tear = [], []
    for k, fs in frags.items():
        total = max(o + l for o, l in fs) + 20
        if k[3] == 1 and total > 65535:
            pod.append((k, total))
        fs2 = sorted(set(fs))                        # 같은 조각이 두 번 잡힌 것은 겹침 아님
        if any(fs2[i + 1][0] < fs2[i][0] + fs2[i][1] and fs2[i + 1][0] != fs2[i][0] for i in range(len(fs2) - 1)):
            tear.append(k)
    if pod:
        k, t = pod[0]
        out.append(Finding(10, 'Ping of Death', '%s → %s 재조립 크기 %d바이트 (>65535) ICMP %d건' % (k[0], k[1], t, len(pod)),
                           ['조각난 ICMP 패킷을 합치면 IP 최대 크기 65,535바이트 초과 → Ping of Death'],
            ev=[p.idx for p in pk if (p.mf or p.frag_off) and (p.src, p.dst, p.ipid, p.proto) == k][:40], wf=[('ip.flags.mf == 1 || ip.frag_offset > 0', 'IP 조각')]))
    elif frags:
        big = [(k, max(o + l for o, l in fs)) for k, fs in frags.items() if k[3] == 1]
        if big and len(big) >= 10:
            out.append(Finding(15, '대용량 조각 ICMP (Ping of Death/Flood 의심)',
                               '조각난 ICMP 묶음 %d개, 최대 %d바이트' % (len(big), max(b for _, b in big)),
                               ['ping -s 65500 류의 큰 ICMP를 반복 전송한 흔적'],
            ev=[p.idx for p in pk if p.proto == 1 and (p.mf or p.frag_off)][:40], wf=[('ip.flags.mf == 1 || ip.frag_offset > 0', 'IP 조각')]))
    if tear:
        out.append(Finding(10, 'Teardrop 공격', 'IP 조각 오프셋 겹침 %d건 (%s → %s)' % (len(tear), tear[0][0], tear[0][1]),
                           ['IP 조각의 오프셋이 서로 겹치게 조작됨 → Teardrop'],
            ev=[p.idx for p in pk if (p.mf or p.frag_off) and (p.src, p.dst, p.ipid, p.proto) == tear[0]][:40], wf=[('ip.flags.mf == 1 || ip.frag_offset > 0', 'IP 조각')]))
    return out


def arp_findings(pk):
    ip_macs = collections.defaultdict(set)
    replies = collections.Counter()
    mac_ips = collections.defaultdict(set)
    asked = set()
    unsol = collections.Counter()
    for p in pk:
        if p.arp:
            op, sha, spa, tha, tpa = p.arp
            if spa != '0.0.0.0':
                ip_macs[spa].add(sha)
            if op == 1:
                asked.add((spa, tpa))
            if op == 2:
                replies[(sha, spa)] += 1
                mac_ips[sha].add(spa)
                if (tpa, spa) not in asked:
                    unsol[(sha, spa)] += 1
    out, done = [], set()

    def ev_of(pred):
        return [p.idx for p in pk if p.arp and pred(p.arp)][:40]
    for ipa, macs in ip_macs.items():
        if len(macs) >= 2:
            # 공격자 MAC = 다른 IP까지 주장하거나, 요청 없는 Reply를 더 많이 보낸 쪽
            att = max(macs, key=lambda m: (len(mac_ips[m]) > 1, unsol[(m, ipa)], replies[(m, ipa)]))
            lines = ['IP %s 에 대해 MAC %d개가 응답: %s' % (ipa, len(macs), ', '.join(sorted(macs)))]
            for (sha, spa), n in replies.most_common():
                if spa == ipa:
                    lines.append('  %s 가 "%s 는 나다" ARP Reply %d회 (요청 없이 보낸 것 %d회)' % (sha, spa, n, unsol[(sha, spa)]))
            lines.append('▶ 공격자 MAC(추정): %s%s' % (att, ('  — 이 MAC은 %s 도 자기라고 주장' % ', '.join(sorted(mac_ips[att] - {ipa})))
                                                   if len(mac_ips[att]) > 1 else ''))
            done.add(att)
            out.append(Finding(10, 'ARP 스푸핑(캐시 포이즈닝)', 'IP %s ↔ MAC %d개, 공격자 MAC %s' % (ipa, len(macs), att), lines,
                ev=ev_of(lambda a: a[2] == ipa), wf=[('arp.src.proto_ipv4 == %s' % ipa, '이 IP를 주장하는 ARP'),
                                                    ('arp.opcode == 2 && arp.src.hw_mac == %s' % att, '공격자 MAC이 보낸 ARP Reply')]))
    for m, ips in mac_ips.items():
        if m in done:
            continue
        n_un = sum(unsol[(m, i)] for i in ips)
        if (len(ips) >= 2 and sum(replies[(m, i)] for i in ips) >= 4) or n_un >= 10:
            lines = ['MAC %s 가 ARP Reply로 IP %s 를 자기 것이라고 주장 (Reply %d회, 요청 없이 보낸 것 %d회)' % (
                m, ', '.join(sorted(ips)), sum(replies[(m, i)] for i in ips), n_un),
                '▶ 공격자 MAC(추정): %s — 피해자 쪽 캡처라 진짜 MAC의 응답은 안 보여도 반복된 가짜 Reply로 판단' % m]
            out.append(Finding(10, 'ARP 스푸핑(캐시 포이즈닝)', '공격자 MAC %s 가 %s 를 사칭 (가짜 ARP Reply %d회)' % (
                m, ', '.join(sorted(ips)), sum(replies[(m, i)] for i in ips)), lines,
                ev=ev_of(lambda a: a[1] == m), wf=[('arp.opcode == 2 && arp.src.hw_mac == %s' % m, '공격자가 보낸 ARP Reply')]))
    return out


def dns_findings(pk, has_other, verbose):
    qs = collections.OrderedDict()
    for p in pk:
        if p.proto == 17 and 53 in (p.sport, p.dport):
            m = parse_dns(p.data)
            if not m or not m['q']:
                continue
            name, qt = m['q'][0]
            e = qs.setdefault((name, qt), {'n': 0, 'resp': None, 'cli': set(), 'srv': set(), 'ts': p.ts, 'ix': []})
            e['ix'].append(p.idx)
            if m['qr'] == 0:
                e['n'] += 1
                e['cli'].add(p.src)
                e['srv'].add(p.dst)
            else:
                e['resp'] = m
    if not qs:
        return []
    user_q = [(k, v) for k, v in qs.items() if not k[0].endswith('in-addr.arpa') and not BROWSER_DOMAINS.search(k[0])]
    browser_q = [(k, v) for k, v in qs.items() if BROWSER_DOMAINS.search(k[0])]
    ptr_q = [(k, v) for k, v in qs.items() if k[0].endswith('in-addr.arpa')]

    def line(k, v):
        if v['resp'] is None:
            r = '응답 없음'
        elif v['resp']['rcode']:
            r = RCODES.get(v['resp']['rcode'], str(v['resp']['rcode']))
        else:
            r = '응답: ' + (', '.join('%s %s' % (t, val) for _, t, val in v['resp']['a']) or '(답 없음)')
        return '%-35s %-5s ×%d  → %s' % (k[0], k[1], v['n'], r)
    lines = ['DNS 서버: %s, 질의한 호스트: %s' % (', '.join(sorted({s for v in qs.values() for s in v['srv']})),
                                          ', '.join(sorted({s for v in qs.values() for s in v['cli']})))]
    for k, v in user_q:
        lines.append('▶ ' + line(k, v))
    if browser_q:
        lines.append('브라우저 자동 질의(Firefox/Chrome 등) %d종%s' % (len(browser_q), ':' if verbose else ' ([v] 자세히 켜면 보임)'))
        if verbose:
            lines += ['   ' + line(k, v) for k, v in browser_q]
    if ptr_q:
        lines.append('역방향(PTR) 조회 %d건 — nmap/traceroute가 IP의 이름을 자동 조회' % len(ptr_q))
        if verbose:
            lines += ['   ' + line(k, v) for k, v in ptr_q]
    names = ', '.join(list(collections.OrderedDict.fromkeys(k[0] for k, _ in user_q))[:4])
    any_resp = any(v['resp'] for _, v in user_q)
    if user_q and not has_other:
        if browser_q:
            title = 'DNS 질의 — 웹 브라우저로 도메인 접속 시도'
            summ = '%s 조회%s (브라우저 질의 동반)' % (names, '' if any_resp else ', DNS 응답 없음 → 접속 실패')
        else:
            title = 'DNS 질의 (nslookup/dig)'
            summ = '%s 조회 → %s' % (names, '응답 있음' if any_resp else '응답 없음')
            lines.append('브라우저/다른 통신 없이 DNS 질의만 존재 → nslookup 등으로 직접 조회')
        return [Finding(65, title, summ, lines,
            ev=[i for _, v in user_q for i in v['ix']], wf=[('dns.qry.name == "%s"' % user_q[0][0][0], '이 도메인 질의'), ('dns', 'DNS 전체')])]
    if user_q or browser_q:
        return [Finding(80, 'DNS 질의', (names or '브라우저 자동 질의') + ' 등 %d종' % len(qs), lines,
            ev=[i for _, v in (user_q or browser_q[:3]) for i in v['ix']], wf=[('dns', 'DNS 전체')])]
    return [Finding(85, 'DNS 역방향 조회', 'PTR %d건 (도구 자동 조회)' % len(ptr_q), lines,
            ev=[i for _, v in ptr_q for i in v['ix']], wf=[('dns.qry.type == 12', 'PTR(역방향) 질의')])]


def flow_summary(pk):
    c = collections.Counter()
    for p in pk:
        if p.src is None:
            continue
        name = {6: 'TCP', 17: 'UDP', 1: 'ICMP'}.get(p.proto, 'IP/%s' % p.proto)
        port = ''
        if p.proto in (6, 17):
            if p.sport is not None and p.dport is not None:
                port = ':%d' % (p.dport if p.dport < p.sport else p.sport)
        c[(p.src, p.dst, name + port)] += 1
    return ['%s → %s  %s  %d개' % (s, d, pr, n) for (s, d, pr), n in c.most_common(8)]


# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 6. 출력
# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
def dispw(s):
    return sum(2 if unicodedata.east_asian_width(ch) in 'WF' else 1 for ch in s)


def pad(s, w):
    return s + ' ' * max(0, w - dispw(s))


def cut(s, w):
    out, cur = '', 0
    for ch in s:
        cw = 2 if unicodedata.east_asian_width(ch) in 'WF' else 1
        if cur + cw > w - 1:
            return out + '…'
        out, cur = out + ch, cur + cw
    return out


# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 7. 화면 출력 (색상, 보고서, 패킷 한 줄 표시)
# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
COLOR = False
_CODES = {'red': '31', 'green': '32', 'yellow': '33', 'blue': '34', 'magenta': '35',
          'cyan': '36', 'bold': '1', 'dim': '2', 'rev': '7'}


def col(s, *codes):
    if not COLOR or not codes:
        return s
    return '\033[%sm%s\033[0m' % (';'.join(_CODES[c] for c in codes), s)


def setup_color(enable):
    global COLOR
    COLOR = bool(enable) and not os.environ.get('NO_COLOR') and sys.stdout.isatty()
    if COLOR and os.name == 'nt':
        os.system('')                       # Windows 콘솔 색상(ANSI) 켜기


def title_color(prio):
    if prio <= 20:
        return ('red', 'bold')
    if prio <= 40:
        return ('yellow', 'bold')
    if prio < 80:
        return ('green', 'bold')
    return ('dim',)


def clear():
    if COLOR:
        print('\033[2J\033[H', end='')
    else:
        print('\n' * 2)


def term_size():
    try:
        import shutil
        s = shutil.get_terminal_size((100, 30))
        return max(30, s.columns), max(10, s.lines)
    except Exception:
        return 100, 30


def main_findings(fs):
    return [x for x in fs if x.prio < 80] or fs[:1]


def render_report(info, findings, verbose=False):
    o = []
    name = os.path.basename(info['path'])
    o.append(col('═' * 78, 'cyan'))
    o.append(col('■ ' + name, 'bold') + '   (패킷 %d개, %s ~ %s, %s)' % (
        info['count'], fmt_ts(info['start']), fmt_ts(info['end'], False),
        '/'.join(LINKTYPES.get(l, str(l)) for l in info['linktypes'])))
    o.append('  판정: ' + ' + '.join(col(f.title, *title_color(f.prio)) for f in main_findings(findings)))
    o.append('─' * 78)
    for f in findings:
        o.append(col('[%s]' % f.title, *title_color(f.prio)) + ' ' + f.summary)
        for l in f.lines:
            o.append('    ' + (col(l, 'bold') if l.lstrip().startswith('▶') else l))
        o.append('')
    if info['noise']:
        o.append(col('  ※ 배경 트래픽(라우터 간 프로토콜 등, 무시 가능): ' +
                     ', '.join('%s %d' % kv for kv in info['noise'].most_common()), 'dim'))
    if verbose:
        o.append('  [통신 요약]')
        o += ['    ' + l for l in info['flows']]
    return o


def row_of(r):
    if r['err']:
        return '오류', r['err']
    mf = main_findings(r['fs'])
    return ' + '.join(x.title for x in mf[:2]), mf[0].summary


def render_table(results, numbered=False, width=None):
    width = width or 400
    names = [os.path.basename(r['path']) for r in results]
    w1 = max([dispw('파일')] + [dispw(n) for n in names]) + 2
    rows = [row_of(r) for r in results]
    w2 = min(30 if width < 120 else 34, max([dispw('판정')] + [dispw(t) for t, _ in rows]) + 2)
    num = '번호 ' if numbered else ''
    o = [num + pad('파일', w1) + pad('판정', w2) + '핵심 내용', '─' * min(width - 1, 110)]
    for i, (r, n, (t, s)) in enumerate(zip(results, names, rows), 1):
        prio = main_findings(r['fs'])[0].prio if r['fs'] else 0
        rest = max(10, width - w1 - w2 - (5 if numbered else 0) - 2)
        o.append(('%3d  ' % i if numbered else '') + pad(n, w1) + col(pad(cut(t, w2 - 1), w2), *title_color(prio)) + cut(s, rest))
    return o


FLAGN = [(0x02, 'SYN'), (0x01, 'FIN'), (0x04, 'RST'), (0x08, 'PSH'), (0x10, 'ACK'), (0x20, 'URG')]
PKT_HEADER = '%6s %11s  %-15s %-15s %-7s %s' % ('No.', 'Time', 'Source', 'Destination', 'Proto', 'Info')


def flag_names(f):
    n = [name for bit, name in FLAGN if f & bit]
    return '[%s]' % ', '.join(n) if n else '[없음]'


def first_line(b, n=70):
    t = b.decode('utf-8', 'replace')
    parts = [x.strip() for x in t.replace('\r', '\n').split('\n') if x.strip()]
    s = ' ↵ '.join(parts[:2]) if parts else ('↵(Enter)' if ('\n' in t or '\r' in t) else '')
    s = ''.join(ch if ch.isprintable() else '.' for ch in s)
    return s[:n] + ('…' if len(s) > n else '')


def proto_label(p):
    if p.arp:
        return 'ARP'
    if p.src is None:
        return p.l2
    if p.proto == 6:
        if p.svc == 'HTTP':
            return 'HTTP' if p.http else 'TCP'
        if p.data:
            for port in (p.sport, p.dport):
                if port in KNOWN_PORTS:
                    return KNOWN_PORTS[port]
        return 'TCP'
    if p.proto == 17:
        if 53 in (p.sport, p.dport):
            return 'DNS'
        return NOISE_UDP.get(p.dport) or NOISE_UDP.get(p.sport) or 'UDP'
    if p.proto == 1:
        return 'ICMP'
    return NOISE_PROTO.get(p.proto, 'IP/%s' % p.proto)


def pkt_info(p):
    if p.arp:
        op, sha, spa, tha, tpa = p.arp
        return 'Who has %s? Tell %s' % (tpa, spa) if op == 1 else '%s is at %s' % (spa, sha)
    if p.src is None:
        return p.l2
    s = ''
    if p.proto == 6 and p.sport is not None:
        s = '%s → %s %s' % (p.sport, p.dport, flag_names(p.flags or 0))
        if p.http:
            m = p.http
            s += ' ' + (('%s %s' % (m['method'], m['uri'])) if 'method' in m else
                        ('HTTP %d %s%s' % (m['code'], m['reason'], ' (%s)' % m['hdr']['content-type'].split(';')[0] if m['hdr'].get('content-type') else '')))
        elif p.data and p.svc == 'HTTP':
            s += ' [재조립되는 TCP 조각] Len=%d %s' % (len(p.data), first_line(p.data, 40) if p.data[:4] in (b'HTTP', b'GET ', b'POST', b'HEAD') else '')
        elif p.data:
            if p.data[:1] == b'\xff':
                rest = strip_iac(p.data)
                s += ' Telnet 옵션 협상(IAC)' + (' ' + first_line(rest) if rest.strip() else '')
            elif p.data[:1].isascii():
                s += ' ' + first_line(p.data)
            else:
                s += ' Len=%d (바이너리/암호화)' % len(p.data)
        else:
            s += ' Win=%s Len=0' % p.win
    elif p.proto == 17 and p.sport is not None:
        if 53 in (p.sport, p.dport):
            m = parse_dns(p.data)
            if m and m['q']:
                s = 'DNS %s %s %s' % ('응답' if m['qr'] else '질의', m['q'][0][1], m['q'][0][0])
                if m['qr']:
                    s += ' → ' + (', '.join(v for _, _, v in m['a']) or RCODES.get(m['rcode'], ''))
        if not s:
            s = '%s → %s Len=%d %s' % (p.sport, p.dport, len(p.data), first_line(p.data, 30) if p.data[:1].isascii() else '')
    elif p.proto == 1 and p.itype is not None:
        s = ICMP_NAMES.get(p.itype, 'type %s' % p.itype)
        if p.itype == 3:
            s += ' (%s)' % UNREACH.get(p.icode, p.icode)
        if p.icmp_seq is not None:
            s += ' id=%d seq=%d' % (p.icmp_id, p.icmp_seq)
        s += ' ttl=%s' % p.ttl
        if p.inner:
            s += ' [원래 패킷: %s → %s:%s]' % (p.inner['src'], p.inner['dst'], p.inner['dport'] or '')
    else:
        s = noise_name(p) or 'IP proto %s' % p.proto
    if p.frag_off or p.mf:
        s += ' [IP 조각 off=%d MF=%d]' % (p.frag_off, p.mf)
    return s


def pkt_line(p, base):
    if p.arp:
        src, dst = p.arp[1], 'Broadcast' if p.arp[0] == 1 else p.arp[3]
    else:
        src, dst = p.src or '', p.dst or ''
    return '%6d %11.6f  %-15s %-15s %-7s %s' % (p.idx, p.ts - base, src, dst, proto_label(p), pkt_info(p))


KEY_RX = re.compile(r'230 |530 |USER |PASS |SYN, ACK|Echo Reply|Time Exceeded|Unreachable|HTTP/1\.|GET |POST |HTTP \d{3}|'
                    r'login|Login|password|Password|DNS 응답|SSH-|is at|uid=|whoami|artex|norma|sqlmap|Nikto|Hydra|'
                    r'%27|UNION|<script|\.\./|/etc/passwd')


def hl(line):
    return col(line, 'bold') if KEY_RX.search(line) else line


# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 8. 보기 화면용 줄 만들기
# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
def key_packet_lines(r, limit=60):
    info, fs = r['info'], r['fs']
    pk, base = info['pkts'], info['start']
    o = [col('■ %s — 중요 패킷' % os.path.basename(r['path']), 'bold'),
         col('  No. = Wireshark 프레임 번호, Time = 첫 패킷 기준 초 (Wireshark 기본 표시와 같음)', 'dim'), '']
    for f in fs:
        o.append(col('▣ ' + f.title, *title_color(f.prio)) + '  ' + f.summary)
        for flt, desc in f.wf:
            o.append('  Wireshark 필터: ' + col(flt, 'cyan') + (col('   ← ' + desc, 'dim') if desc else ''))
        groups = f.groups or [(None, f.ev)]
        if not any(g for _, g in groups):
            o.append(col('   (표시할 패킷 없음)', 'dim'))
        for label, idxs in groups:
            ev = sorted(set(i for i in idxs if 1 <= i <= len(pk)))
            if not ev:
                continue
            if label:
                o.append(col('  ▸ ' + label, 'magenta'))
            o.append(col(PKT_HEADER, 'dim'))
            for i in ev[:limit]:
                o.append(hl(pkt_line(pk[i - 1], base)))
            if len(ev) > limit:
                o.append(col('   … %d개 더 있음 ([2] 전체 목록 또는 위 필터로 확인)' % (len(ev) - limit), 'dim'))
        o.append('')
    return o


def all_packet_lines(r, show_noise=False):
    pk, base = r['info']['pkts'], r['info']['start']
    o = [col('■ %s — 전체 패킷 목록' % os.path.basename(r['path']), 'bold'), col(PKT_HEADER, 'dim')]
    hidden = 0
    for p in pk:
        n = noise_name(p)
        if n and not show_noise:
            hidden += 1
            continue
        line = pkt_line(p, base)
        o.append(col(line + '  (배경)', 'dim') if n else hl(line))
    if hidden:
        o.append(col('  (배경 트래픽 %d개 숨김 — OSPF/CDP/SLARP 등)' % hidden, 'dim'))
    return o


def follow_lines(r, maxbytes=4000):
    conns = [c for c in r['info']['conns'] if c.bytes[0] or c.bytes[1]]
    o = [col('■ %s — TCP 대화 내용 (Wireshark Follow TCP Stream과 같음)' % os.path.basename(r['path']), 'bold'),
         col('  C> 클라이언트가 보낸 것,  S< 서버가 보낸 것,  [#번호] 프레임', 'dim'), '']
    if not conns:
        return o + ['(데이터가 있는 TCP 세션 없음)']
    for n, c in enumerate(conns, 1):
        ok = (c.service == 'FTP' and re.search(rb'(^|\n)230 ', c.stream(1))) or \
             (c.service in ('POP3', 'IMAP', 'SMTP', 'Telnet') and re.search(rb'(?i)\+OK.*logged|OK LOGIN|235 |last login', c.stream(1)))
        o.append(col('━━ 세션 %d: %s → %s [%s] %s  (클라 %dB / 서버 %dB) ━━%s' % (
            n, ep(c.cli), ep(c.srv), c.service or identify_service(c), fmt_ts(c.start, False), c.bytes[0], c.bytes[1],
            '  ★ 로그인 성공' if ok else ''), 'green' if ok else 'cyan', *(('bold',) if ok else ())))
        shown = 0
        for ts, d, data, ix in c.timeline():
            if shown >= maxbytes:
                o.append(col('   … (생략)', 'dim'))
                break
            if c.service in ('SSH', 'TLS(HTTPS 등)') and not data.startswith(b'SSH-'):
                txt = '(암호화 데이터 %dB)' % len(data)
            else:
                txt = data.decode('utf-8', 'replace').replace('\r', '')
                txt = ''.join(ch if ch.isprintable() or ch in '\n\t' else '.' for ch in txt)
            for k, l in enumerate(txt.rstrip('\n').split('\n')):
                tag = ('C> ' if d == 0 else 'S< ')
                o.append(col('  %s' % tag, 'blue' if d == 0 else 'red') + l + (col('  [#%d]' % ix, 'dim') if k == 0 else ''))
            shown += len(data)
        o.append('')
    return o


def frame_detail_lines(r, n):
    info = r['info']
    if not 1 <= n <= len(info['pkts']):
        return ['프레임 번호는 1 ~ %d 사이여야 합니다.' % len(info['pkts'])]
    p = info['pkts'][n - 1]
    raw = info['raw'][n - 1][2]
    o = [col('■ %s — 프레임 #%d 상세' % (os.path.basename(r['path']), n), 'bold'),
         col(PKT_HEADER, 'dim'), pkt_line(p, info['start']), '',
         '  시간: %s (첫 패킷부터 %.6f초)   길이: %d바이트   링크: %s' % (
             fmt_ts(p.ts), p.ts - info['start'], len(raw), LINKTYPES.get(info['raw'][n - 1][1], '?'))]
    if p.src:
        o.append('  IP: %s → %s   TTL=%s  ID=%s  프로토콜=%s  전체길이=%s' % (p.src, p.dst, p.ttl, p.ipid, p.proto, p.iplen))
    if p.proto == 6 and p.sport is not None:
        o.append('  TCP: %s → %s  %s  Seq=%s Ack=%s Win=%s  옵션=%s  데이터 %d바이트' % (
            p.sport, p.dport, flag_names(p.flags or 0), p.seq, p.ack, p.win, p.opts.hex() or '없음', len(p.data)))
    elif p.proto == 17 and p.sport is not None:
        o.append('  UDP: %s → %s  데이터 %d바이트' % (p.sport, p.dport, len(p.data)))
    elif p.proto == 1 and p.itype is not None:
        o.append('  ICMP: type=%s(%s) code=%s' % (p.itype, ICMP_NAMES.get(p.itype, '?'), p.icode))
    if p.data:
        o.append('  데이터(텍스트): ' + preview(p.data, 200))
    o.append('')
    o.append(col('  [16진수 덤프 — 링크 계층 헤더 포함 원본]', 'dim'))
    for off in range(0, len(raw), 16):
        ch = raw[off:off + 16]
        hx = ' '.join('%02x' % x for x in ch)
        asc = ''.join(chr(x) if 32 <= x < 127 else '.' for x in ch)
        o.append('  %04x  %-48s  %s' % (off, hx, asc))
    return o


def grep_lines(results, pattern):
    try:
        rx = re.compile(pattern.encode('utf-8'), re.I)
    except re.error as e:
        return ['정규식 오류: %s' % e]
    o = [col('■ 검색: "%s"' % pattern, 'bold'), col('  파일  ' + PKT_HEADER, 'dim')]
    hits = 0
    for r in results:
        if r['err']:
            continue
        for p in r['info']['pkts']:
            raw = r['info']['raw'][p.idx - 1][2]
            blob = p.data if p.src else raw
            if blob and rx.search(blob):
                hits += 1
                o.append('  %-8s ' % os.path.basename(r['path']) + hl(pkt_line(p, r['info']['start'])))
    o.append(col('  → %d개 패킷 일치' % hits, 'bold'))
    return o


# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 8-2. 추가 기능: 계정 모음 / Wireshark 필터 / 통계 / 파일 추출 / 답안
# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
def collect_auth_creds(svc, cip, sip, sport, results, brute):
    out = []
    for r in results:
        for a in r['attempts']:
            out.append({'svc': svc, 'cli': cip, 'srv': '%s:%d' % (sip, sport), 'user': a['user'],
                        'pass': a['pass'], 'result': a['result'] or '응답없음', 'frames': a['ix'],
                        'brute': brute, 'ts': a['ts']})
    return out


def collect_http_creds(cip, sip, sport, cs, brute):
    out = []
    for c in cs:
        for q, rsp in analyze_http(c):
            ua = q['hdr'].get('user-agent', '')
            owner = next((t for rx, t, d in UA_SIGS if ua and re.search(rx, ua)), None)
            if owner in SCANNER_TOOLS or owner in DIRBUST_TOOLS or owner in ('sqlmap', 'nmap NSE'):
                continue                      # 스캐너가 시험 삼아 넣은 값은 제외
            for kind, u, pw in http_creds(q):
                if not pw:
                    continue
                res = getattr(c, '_brute_res', {}).get(id(q)) if brute else None
                out.append({'svc': 'HTTP(%s)' % kind, 'cli': cip, 'srv': '%s:%d' % (sip, sport), 'user': u, 'pass': pw,
                            'result': res or http_login_result(rsp, q),
                            'frames': [x['frame'] for x in (q, rsp) if x], 'brute': brute, 'ts': c.start})
    return out


def creds_lines(results, show_all=False):
    o = [col('■ 계정 / 비밀번호 모아보기 (모든 파일)', 'bold'),
         col('  평문 프로토콜(FTP/Telnet/HTTP/POP3/IMAP/SMTP)에서 찾은 로그인 정보. SSH/HTTPS는 암호화라 안 보임', 'dim'), '']
    any_found = False
    for r in results:
        if r['err'] or not r['info'].get('creds'):
            continue
        cr = r['info']['creds']
        any_found = True
        o.append(col('▣ ' + os.path.basename(r['path']), 'bold'))
        normal = [c for c in cr if not c['brute']]
        brute = [c for c in cr if c['brute']]
        for c in normal:
            o.append('  %-10s %s → %-21s 계정 %-12s 비밀번호 %-14s → %s  %s' % (
                c['svc'], c['cli'], c['srv'], c['user'], c['pass'], res_col(c['result']),
                col('#' + ','.join(map(str, c['frames'][:3])), 'dim') if c['frames'] else ''))
        if brute:
            ok = [c for c in brute if '성공' in str(c['result'])]
            o.append('  %s 무차별 대입 %d회 시도 (계정 %d개 × 비밀번호 %d개)' % (
                brute[0]['svc'], len(brute), len({c['user'] for c in brute}), len({c['pass'] for c in brute})))
            for c in (brute if show_all else ok):
                o.append('    %s 계정 %-12s 비밀번호 %-14s → %s  %s' % (
                    '★' if '성공' in str(c['result']) else ' ', c['user'], c['pass'], res_col(c['result']),
                    col('#' + ','.join(map(str, c['frames'][:3])), 'dim') if c['frames'] else ''))
            if not show_all:
                o.append(col('    (전체 시도 목록은 파일을 열고 [v] 자세히 → 보고서에서 확인)', 'dim'))
        o.append('')
    if not any_found:
        o.append('  평문 로그인 정보가 있는 파일이 없습니다.')
    return o


def res_col(s):
    s = str(s)
    if s == '성공' or s.startswith('2'):
        return col(s, 'green', 'bold')
    if s == '실패' or s.startswith(('4', '5')):
        return col(s, 'red')
    return s


# ── 미니 Wireshark 디스플레이 필터 ───────────────────────────────────
class FilterError(Exception):
    pass


def _ftp_parts(p):
    if p.proto == 6 and p.data and 21 in (p.sport, p.dport):
        line = p.data.split(b'\n', 1)[0].decode('utf-8', 'replace').strip('\r ')
        if p.dport == 21:
            cmd, _, arg = line.partition(' ')
            return ('req', cmd.upper(), arg)
        m = re.match(r'(\d{3})[ -]?(.*)', line)
        if m:
            return ('resp', int(m.group(1)), m.group(2))
    return None


def _http_parts(p):
    m = p.http
    if not m:
        return None
    if 'method' in m:
        return {'method': m['method'], 'uri': m['uri'], 'hdr': m['hdr']}
    return {'code': m['code'], 'hdr': m['hdr']}


def _l4ok(p):
    return p.reasm or not (p.mf or p.frag_off)


def _inn(p, key, proto=None):
    i = p.inner
    if not i or (proto is not None and i['proto'] != proto) or i.get(key) is None:
        return []
    return [i[key]]


def _dns(p):
    if p.proto == 17 and 53 in (p.sport, p.dport) and len(p.data) >= 12 and _l4ok(p):
        return parse_dns(p.data)
    i = p.inner
    if i and i['proto'] == 17 and 53 in (i['sport'], i['dport']) and len(i.get('udata') or b'') >= 12:
        try:
            return parse_dns(i['udata'])
        except Exception:
            return None
    return None


_QT_NUM = {v: k for k, v in QTYPES.items()}


def _tcp(p, v):
    return [v] if p.proto == 6 and p.sport is not None and _l4ok(p) else []


def _udp(p, v):
    return [v] if p.proto == 17 and p.sport is not None and _l4ok(p) else []


def _flag(bit):
    return lambda p: [int(bool((p.flags or 0) & bit))] if p.proto == 6 and p.sport is not None and _l4ok(p) else []


def _icmp(p, v):
    return [v] if p.proto == 1 and v is not None and _l4ok(p) else []


def _protocols(p):
    """Wireshark frame.protocols 와 비슷한 'eth:ethertype:ip:tcp:ftp' 문자열"""
    out = [{'Ethernet': 'eth:ethertype'}.get(p.l2, '')] if p.eth_dst else []
    if p.l2 in ('ipv4', 'ipv6'):
        out.append('ip' if p.l2 == 'ipv4' else 'ipv6')
        l4 = {6: 'tcp', 17: 'udp', 1: 'icmp'}.get(p.proto)
        if l4 and _l4ok(p):
            out.append(l4)
            for name in ('http', 'ftp', 'ftp-data', 'ssh', 'telnet', 'smtp', 'pop', 'imap', 'dns', 'tls'):
                try:
                    if PROTOS[name](p):
                        out.append(name)
                        break
                except Exception:
                    pass
    elif p.arp:
        out.append('arp')
    else:
        out.append(str(p.l2 or '').lower())
    if _is_data(p):
        out.append('data')
    return ':'.join(x for x in out if x)


def _tcp_opt(p, kind, conv):
    if p.proto != 6 or not p.opts or not _l4ok(p):
        return []
    o, b = 0, p.opts
    while o < len(b):
        k = b[o]
        if k == 0:
            break
        if k == 1:
            o += 1
            continue
        if o + 1 >= len(b):
            break
        ln = b[o + 1]
        if ln < 2:
            break
        if k == kind:
            try:
                return [conv(b[o + 2:o + ln])]
            except (struct.error, IndexError):
                return []
        o += ln
    return []


_TLS_PORTS = {443, 8443, 993, 995, 465, 636, 989, 990, 5061, 853, 4433}


def _tls_hs(p):
    return p.proto == 6 and len(p.data) >= 6 and p.data[0] == 0x16 and p.data[1] == 3 and _l4ok(p) and bool({p.sport, p.dport} & _TLS_PORTS)


def _sni_of(p):
    if not _tls_hs(p) or p.data[5] != 1:
        return None
    b = p.data
    try:
        q = 5 + 4 + 2 + 32
        q += 1 + b[q]
        q += 2 + struct.unpack('>H', b[q:q + 2])[0]
        q += 1 + b[q]
        end = q + 2 + struct.unpack('>H', b[q:q + 2])[0]
        q += 2
        while q + 4 <= min(end, len(b)):
            t, ln = struct.unpack('>HH', b[q:q + 4])
            if t == 0:
                n = struct.unpack('>H', b[q + 7:q + 9])[0]
                return b[q + 9:q + 9 + n].decode('ascii', 'replace')
            q += 4 + ln
    except (struct.error, IndexError):
        return None
    return None


_APP_PORTS = {21, 22, 23, 25, 53, 80, 110, 143, 443, 587, 8080}


def _is_data(p):
    """Wireshark 'data' = 어떤 프로토콜로도 해석 못 한 페이로드"""
    if (p.mf or p.frag_off) and not p.reasm and p.data:
        return True                                    # 아직 다 모이지 않은 IP 조각
    if p.proto == 1 and p.itype in (0, 8) and p.data and _l4ok(p):
        return True
    if p.inner and p.inner.get('udata') and min(p.inner['sport'], p.inner['dport']) >= 1024:
        return True                                    # ICMP 오류 안에 담긴 원래 UDP 데이터
    if not p.data or p.src is None or not _l4ok(p) or p.proto not in (6, 17):
        return False
    if p.proto == 17:
        return min(p.sport, p.dport) >= 1024 and not ({p.sport, p.dport} & {1900, 5353, 5355, 3478, 4789, 5060})
    if p.http is not None or p.sport in _APP_PORTS or p.dport in _APP_PORTS or p.svc == 'FTP-DATA' or p.retrans:
        return False
    return not p.data[:4] in (b'SSH-', b'HTTP')


FIELDS = {
    'frame.number': lambda p: [p.idx],
    'frame.len': lambda p: [p.wlen or p.flen],
    'frame.cap_len': lambda p: [p.flen],
    'eth.src': lambda p: [p.eth_src] if p.eth_dst else [],
    'eth.dst': lambda p: [p.eth_dst] if p.eth_dst else [],
    'eth.addr': lambda p: [p.eth_src, p.eth_dst] if p.eth_dst else [],
    'ip.flags.df': lambda p: ([int(bool(p.ipdf))] + _inn(p, 'df')) if p.src and p.l2 == 'ipv4' else [],
    'tcp.stream': lambda p: [p.stream] if p.stream is not None else [],
    'tcp.options': lambda p: [p.opts] if p.proto == 6 and p.opts and _l4ok(p) else [],
    'frame.protocols': lambda p: [_protocols(p)],
    'http.request.full_uri': lambda p: ['http://%s%s' % (x['hdr'].get('host', p.dst), x['uri']) for x in [_http_parts(p)] if x and 'uri' in x],
    'tcp.analysis.retransmission': lambda p: [1] if p.retrans and p.proto == 6 else [],
    'tcp.options.timestamp.tsval': lambda p: _tcp_opt(p, 8, lambda v: struct.unpack('>I', v[:4])[0]),
    'tcp.options.timestamp.tsecr': lambda p: _tcp_opt(p, 8, lambda v: struct.unpack('>I', v[4:8])[0]),
    'tcp.options.mss_val': lambda p: _tcp_opt(p, 2, lambda v: struct.unpack('>H', v[:2])[0]),
    'tcp.options.wscale.shift': lambda p: _tcp_opt(p, 3, lambda v: v[0]),
    'tls.handshake.type': lambda p: [p.data[5]] if _tls_hs(p) else [],
    'tls.handshake.extensions_server_name': lambda p: [x for x in [_sni_of(p)] if x],
    'tls.record.content_type': lambda p: [p.data[0]] if PROTOS['tls'](p) else [],
    'tcp.hdr_len': lambda p: _tcp(p, 20 + len(p.opts)),
    'tcp.seq_raw': lambda p: _tcp(p, p.seq),
    'tcp.ack_raw': lambda p: _tcp(p, p.ack),
    'tcp.nxtseq': lambda p: _tcp(p, None if p.rseq is None else p.rseq + len(p.data) + (1 if (p.flags or 0) & 3 else 0)),
    'frame': lambda p: [p.rawb],
    'ip.src': lambda p: ([p.src] + _inn(p, 'src')) if p.src else [],
    'ip.dst': lambda p: ([p.dst] + _inn(p, 'dst')) if p.src else [],
    'ip.addr': lambda p: ([p.src, p.dst] + _inn(p, 'src') + _inn(p, 'dst')) if p.src else [],
    'ip.ttl': lambda p: ([p.ttl] + _inn(p, 'ttl')) if p.src else [],
    'ip.proto': lambda p: ([p.proto] + _inn(p, 'proto')) if p.src else [],
    'ip.id': lambda p: ([p.ipid] + _inn(p, 'id')) if p.ipid is not None else [],
    'ip.len': lambda p: ([p.iplen] + _inn(p, 'len')) if p.src else [],
    'ip.flags.mf': lambda p: [int(p.mf)] if p.src else [],
    'ip.frag_offset': lambda p: [p.frag_off] if p.src else [],
    'tcp': lambda p: [p.data] if p.proto == 6 and p.sport is not None and _l4ok(p) else [],
    'tcp.port': lambda p: ([p.sport, p.dport] if p.proto == 6 and p.sport is not None and _l4ok(p) else [])
                          + _inn(p, 'sport', 6) + _inn(p, 'dport', 6),
    'tcp.srcport': lambda p: _tcp(p, p.sport) + _inn(p, 'sport', 6),
    'tcp.dstport': lambda p: _tcp(p, p.dport) + _inn(p, 'dport', 6),
    'tcp.flags': lambda p: _tcp(p, p.flags),
    'tcp.flags.syn': _flag(0x02), 'tcp.flags.ack': _flag(0x10), 'tcp.flags.fin': _flag(0x01),
    'tcp.flags.reset': _flag(0x04), 'tcp.flags.push': _flag(0x08), 'tcp.flags.urg': _flag(0x20),
    'tcp.len': lambda p: _tcp(p, len(p.data)),
    'tcp.seq': lambda p: _tcp(p, p.rseq),
    'tcp.ack': lambda p: _tcp(p, p.rack),
    'tcp.window_size': lambda p: _tcp(p, p.win if p.wscale is None else p.win << p.wscale),
    'tcp.window_size_value': lambda p: _tcp(p, p.win),
    'tcp.payload': lambda p: [p.data] if p.proto == 6 and p.data else [],
    'udp': lambda p: ([p.data] if p.proto == 17 and p.sport is not None and _l4ok(p) else []) + _inn(p, 'udata', 17),
    'udp.port': lambda p: ([p.sport, p.dport] if p.proto == 17 and p.sport is not None and _l4ok(p) else [])
                          + _inn(p, 'sport', 17) + _inn(p, 'dport', 17),
    'udp.srcport': lambda p: _udp(p, p.sport) + _inn(p, 'sport', 17),
    'udp.dstport': lambda p: _udp(p, p.dport) + _inn(p, 'dport', 17),
    'udp.length': lambda p: _udp(p, len(p.data) + 8) + _inn(p, 'ulen', 17),
    'icmp': lambda p: [p.data] if p.proto == 1 and p.itype is not None and _l4ok(p) else [],
    'icmp.type': lambda p: ([p.itype] if p.proto == 1 and p.itype is not None and _l4ok(p) else []) + _inn(p, 'itype', 1),
    'icmp.code': lambda p: _icmp(p, p.icode if p.itype is not None else None) + _inn(p, 'icode', 1),
    'icmp.seq': lambda p: _icmp(p, p.icmp_seq) + _inn(p, 'icmp_seq', 1),
    'icmp.ident': lambda p: _icmp(p, p.icmp_id) + _inn(p, 'icmp_id', 1),
    'arp.opcode': lambda p: [p.arp[0]] if p.arp else [],
    'arp.src.hw_mac': lambda p: [p.arp[1]] if p.arp else [],
    'arp.src.proto_ipv4': lambda p: [p.arp[2]] if p.arp else [],
    'arp.dst.hw_mac': lambda p: [p.arp[3]] if p.arp else [],
    'arp.dst.proto_ipv4': lambda p: [p.arp[4]] if p.arp else [],
    'dns.qry.name': lambda p: [q[0] for q in (_dns(p) or {}).get('q', [])],
    'dns.qry.type': lambda p: [_QT_NUM.get(q[1], q[1]) for q in (_dns(p) or {}).get('q', [])],
    'dns.flags.response': lambda p: [(_dns(p) or {}).get('qr')] if _dns(p) else [],
    'dns.flags.rcode': lambda p: [(_dns(p) or {}).get('rcode')] if _dns(p) else [],
    'dns.a': lambda p: [v for _, t, v in (_dns(p) or {}).get('a', []) if t == 'A'],
    'ftp.request.command': lambda p: [x[1] for x in [_ftp_parts(p)] if x and x[0] == 'req'],
    'ftp.request.arg': lambda p: [x[2] for x in [_ftp_parts(p)] if x and x[0] == 'req'],
    'ftp.response.code': lambda p: [x[1] for x in [_ftp_parts(p)] if x and x[0] == 'resp'],
    'ftp.response.arg': lambda p: [x[2] for x in [_ftp_parts(p)] if x and x[0] == 'resp'],
    'http.request.method': lambda p: [x['method'] for x in [_http_parts(p)] if x and 'method' in x],
    'http.request.uri': lambda p: [x['uri'] for x in [_http_parts(p)] if x and 'uri' in x],
    'http.host': lambda p: [x['hdr'].get('host') for x in [_http_parts(p)] if x and 'host' in x['hdr']],
    'http.user_agent': lambda p: [x['hdr'].get('user-agent') for x in [_http_parts(p)] if x and 'user-agent' in x['hdr']],
    'http.response.code': lambda p: [x['code'] for x in [_http_parts(p)] if x and 'code' in x],
    'http.server': lambda p: [x['hdr'].get('server') for x in [_http_parts(p)] if x and 'server' in x['hdr']],
    'http.cookie': lambda p: [x['hdr'].get('cookie') for x in [_http_parts(p)] if x and 'cookie' in x['hdr']],
    'http.authorization': lambda p: [x['hdr'].get('authorization') for x in [_http_parts(p)] if x and 'authorization' in x['hdr']],
}
FIELDS['http.request'] = lambda p: [1] if (_http_parts(p) or {}).get('method') else []
FIELDS['http.response'] = lambda p: [1] if 'code' in (_http_parts(p) or {}) else []
FIELDS['ssh.protocol'] = lambda p: [first_line(p.data)] if p.proto == 6 and p.data.startswith(b'SSH-') else []


def _port_proto(*ports):
    return lambda p: p.proto == 6 and bool(p.data) and not p.retrans and (p.sport in ports or p.dport in ports)


PROTOS = {
    'ip': lambda p: p.l2 == 'ipv4', 'ipv6': lambda p: p.l2 == 'ipv6',
    'tcp': lambda p: (p.proto == 6 and _l4ok(p)) or bool(_inn(p, 'proto', 6)),
    'udp': lambda p: (p.proto == 17 and _l4ok(p)) or bool(_inn(p, 'proto', 17)),
    'icmp': lambda p: p.proto == 1 and p.itype is not None and _l4ok(p), 'arp': lambda p: bool(p.arp),
    'dns': lambda p: _dns(p) is not None or (bool(p.inner) and p.inner['proto'] == 17 and 53 in (p.inner['sport'], p.inner['dport'])),
    'ftp': _port_proto(21), 'ssh': _port_proto(22), 'telnet': _port_proto(23),
    'smtp': _port_proto(25, 587), 'pop': _port_proto(110), 'imap': _port_proto(143),
    'http': lambda p: p.http is not None,
    'tls': lambda p: p.proto == 6 and len(p.data) >= 5 and 0x14 <= p.data[0] <= 0x17 and p.data[1] == 3 and _l4ok(p)
    and bool({p.sport, p.dport} & _TLS_PORTS),
    'ftp-data': lambda p: p.proto == 6 and bool(p.data) and p.svc == 'FTP-DATA',
    'ospf': lambda p: p.proto == 89, 'eigrp': lambda p: p.proto == 88,
    'cdp': lambda p: p.l2 == 'CDP', 'slarp': lambda p: p.l2 == 'SLARP',
    'data': _is_data,
}

_TOK = re.compile(r'\s*(?:(\(|\)|\{|\}|,)|(&&|\|\||==|!=|>=|<=|>|<|!|~|===|!==)|("(?:[^"\\]|\\.)*")|([A-Za-z0-9_.:/\-]+))')
_WORDOPS = {'and': '&&', 'or': '||', 'not': '!', 'eq': '==', 'ne': '!=', 'gt': '>', 'lt': '<', 'ge': '>=',
            'le': '<=', 'contains': 'contains', 'matches': 'matches', 'in': 'in'}


def _unescape(t):
    """필터 문자열의 escape 처리 → bytes (백슬래시 x 두자리, 8진수 세자리, n t r). 모르는 escape 는 글자 그대로 (C:\\Users 등)"""
    out, i = bytearray(), 0
    while i < len(t):
        ch = t[i]
        if ch == '\\' and i + 1 < len(t):
            n = t[i + 1]
            if n in '\\"\'':
                out += n.encode()
                i += 2
                continue
            if n in 'ntr':
                out += {'n': b'\n', 't': b'\t', 'r': b'\r'}[n]
                i += 2
                continue
            if n == 'x' and re.match(r'[0-9a-fA-F]{2}', t[i + 2:i + 4]):
                out.append(int(t[i + 2:i + 4], 16))
                i += 4
                continue
            if re.match(r'[0-7]{3}', t[i + 1:i + 4]):
                out.append(int(t[i + 1:i + 4], 8) & 0xff)
                i += 4
                continue
        out += ch.encode('utf-8')
        i += 1
    return bytes(out)


def _tokens(s):
    pos, out = 0, []
    s = s.strip()
    while pos < len(s):
        m = _TOK.match(s, pos)
        if not m or m.end() == pos:
            raise FilterError('이해할 수 없는 부분: "%s"' % s[pos:pos + 15])
        pos = m.end()
        if m.group(1):
            out.append(('p', m.group(1)))
        elif m.group(2):
            out.append(('op', m.group(2)))
        elif m.group(3):
            out.append(('str', _unescape(m.group(3)[1:-1])))
        else:
            w = m.group(4)
            out.append(('op', _WORDOPS[w.lower()]) if w.lower() in _WORDOPS else ('word', w))
    return out


_HEXBYTES = re.compile(r'^[0-9a-fA-F]{2}([:.\-][0-9a-fA-F]{2})+$')


def _conv(v, raw):
    """비교값을 필드값 형식에 맞게 변환 (값은 compile 때 이미 검사됨)"""
    if isinstance(raw, (bytes, bytearray)):
        if isinstance(v, str):
            return bytes.fromhex(re.sub(r'[:.\-]', '', v)) if _HEXBYTES.match(v) else v.encode('utf-8')
        return v
    if isinstance(v, (bytes, bytearray)):
        v = v.decode('utf-8', 'replace')
    if isinstance(raw, int) and not isinstance(raw, bool):
        try:
            return _num(v)
        except ValueError:
            return v
    return v


def _num(v):
    t = str(v).strip().lower()
    if t in ('true', 'false'):
        return int(t == 'true')
    if re.match(r'^0[0-7]+$', t):
        return int(t, 8)
    return int(t, 0)


def _ipval(a):
    import ipaddress
    try:
        return ipaddress.ip_address(a)
    except ValueError:
        return None


def _eq(a, b):
    if isinstance(a, str) and isinstance(b, str) and '/' in b and a.count('.') == 3:
        try:
            import ipaddress
            return ipaddress.ip_address(a) in ipaddress.ip_network(b, strict=False)
        except ValueError:
            pass
    if isinstance(a, str) and isinstance(b, str):
        return a.lower() == b.lower() if ':' in a else a == b
    return a == b


def _cmp(op, a, b):
    try:
        if op == '==':
            return _eq(a, b)
        if op in ('>', '<', '>=', '<=') and isinstance(a, str) and isinstance(b, str):
            ia, ib = _ipval(a), _ipval(b)
            if ia is not None and ib is not None:
                a, b = ia, ib
        if op == '>':
            return a > b
        if op == '<':
            return a < b
        if op == '>=':
            return a >= b
        if op == '<=':
            return a <= b
        if op == 'contains':
            if isinstance(a, (bytes, bytearray)):
                return b in a
            return str(b) in str(a)
    except Exception:
        return False
    return False


# 숫자 필드가 아닌 것들 (나머지는 숫자로 비교)
_STR_FIELDS = {'ip.src', 'ip.dst', 'ip.addr', 'arp.src.hw_mac', 'arp.dst.hw_mac', 'arp.src.proto_ipv4', 'arp.dst.proto_ipv4',
               'eth.src', 'eth.dst', 'eth.addr', 'dns.qry.name', 'dns.a', 'ftp.request.command', 'ftp.request.arg',
               'ftp.response.arg', 'http.request.method', 'http.request.uri', 'http.host', 'http.user_agent', 'http.server',
               'http.cookie', 'http.authorization', 'ssh.protocol', 'frame', 'tcp', 'udp', 'icmp', 'tcp.payload'}
_IP_FIELDS = {'ip.src', 'ip.dst', 'ip.addr', 'arp.src.proto_ipv4', 'arp.dst.proto_ipv4', 'dns.a'}


def _check_value(name, op, v):
    """Wireshark처럼 잘못된 값은 바로 오류로 알려줌"""
    if isinstance(v, (bytes, bytearray)):
        v = v.decode('utf-8', 'replace')
    if op in ('contains', 'matches', '~'):
        return
    if name in _IP_FIELDS:
        import ipaddress
        try:
            if '/' in v:
                ipaddress.ip_network(v, strict=False)
            else:
                ipaddress.ip_address(v)
        except ValueError:
            raise FilterError('"%s" 는 올바른 IP 주소가 아닙니다' % v)
        return
    if name not in _STR_FIELDS:
        try:
            _num(v)
        except ValueError:
            raise FilterError('%s 에는 숫자가 와야 합니다 ("%s" 는 숫자가 아님)' % (name, v))


def compile_filter(text):
    try:
        e = _compile_filter(text)
        return lambda p: e(getattr(p, 'rview', None) or p)
    except FilterError:
        raise
    except RecursionError:
        raise FilterError('괄호나 ! 가 너무 많이 겹쳐 있습니다')
    except Exception as e:
        raise FilterError('필터를 이해할 수 없습니다 (%s)' % e)


def _compile_filter(text):
    toks = _tokens(text)
    if not toks:
        raise FilterError('필터가 비어 있습니다')
    pos = [0]

    def peek():
        return toks[pos[0]] if pos[0] < len(toks) else (None, None)

    def take():
        t = peek()
        pos[0] += 1
        return t

    def p_or():
        left = p_and()
        while peek() == ('op', '||'):
            take()
            right = p_and()
            left = (lambda l, r: lambda p: l(p) or r(p))(left, right)
        return left

    def p_and():
        left = p_not()
        while peek() == ('op', '&&'):
            take()
            right = p_not()
            left = (lambda l, r: lambda p: l(p) and r(p))(left, right)
        return left

    def p_not():
        if peek() == ('op', '!'):
            take()
            inner = p_not()
            return lambda p: not inner(p)
        return p_primary()

    def value():
        k, v = take()
        if k in ('word', 'str'):
            return v
        raise FilterError('비교할 값이 와야 합니다')

    def p_primary():
        k, v = take()
        if (k, v) == ('p', '('):
            e = p_or()
            if take() != ('p', ')'):
                raise FilterError('괄호 ")" 가 빠졌습니다')
            return e
        if k != 'word':
            raise FilterError('필드 이름이 와야 할 자리에 "%s"' % v)
        name = v.lower()
        nk, nv = peek()
        is_cmp = nk == 'op' and nv not in ('&&', '||', '!')
